	public HagedornBrownLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (flowRegCorr != null)
		{
			pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		return CalculateLiquidHoldUp(pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (base.FlowStream == null || base.Diameter == 0.0)
		{
			return result;
		}
		if (pattern == FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown)
		{
			DunsAndRosFlowRegimeCorrelation dunsAndRosFlowRegimeCorrelation = new DunsAndRosFlowRegimeCorrelation(base.FlowStream, base.Diameter, base.InclinedAngle, base.Roughness);
			pattern = dunsAndRosFlowRegimeCorrelation.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		switch (pattern)
		{
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Liquid:
			result = 1.0;
			break;
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Vapour:
			result = 0.0;
			break;
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Bubble:
		{
			double num = 0.24;
			double num2 = 1.0 + (LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true) + GasSupVelocity()) / num;
			double num3 = num2 * num2 - 4.0 * GasSupVelocity() / num;
			if (num3 > 0.0)
			{
				result = 1.0 - 0.5 * (num2 - Math.Sqrt(num3));
			}
			break;
		}
		default:
		{
			double pNGV = GasVelocityNumber();
			double pNLV = LiquidVelocityNumber();
			double pNLE = LiquidViscosityNumber(combinedPhase: true);
			double pND = PipeDiameterNumber(combinedPhase: true);
			double value = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
			double psiParameter = GetPsiParameter(pNGV, pNLE, pND);
			double holdUpPsiParameter = GetHoldUpPsiParameter(pNGV, pNLV, pNLE, pND, value);
			result = holdUpPsiParameter * psiParameter;
			result = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, result));
			break;
		}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateWaterHoldUp()
	{
		return RealProperty.EMPTY;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (flowRegCorr != null)
		{
			pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		return CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double num = CalculateLiquidHoldUp(pattern);
		double fEMPTY = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (num == 1.0)
		{
			return base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
		}
		if (num == 0.0)
		{
			return base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Density.Value;
		}
		IPhase phaseByType = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		fEMPTY = (1.0 - num) * phaseByType.Density.Value + num * combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
		double val = CombinedLiquidHomogeneousDensity();
		return Math.Max(fEMPTY, val);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (flowRegCorr != null)
		{
			pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		return CalculateEquivalentDensity(pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		return CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
	}

	internal double GetViscosityCoefficient(double PNLE)
	{
		double val = Math.Log10(PNLE);
		val = Math.Min(-0.466, val);
		double num = (0.0 - (2.01 + val * (2.81 + val * 2.75))) / (0.9344 + val * (1.057 + val));
		if (val < -2.0)
		{
			num = Math.Max(-2.7, num);
		}
		return Math.Pow(10.0, num);
	}

	internal double GetPsiParameter(double PNGV, double PNLE, double PND)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double val = PNGV * Math.Pow(PNLE, 0.38) / Math.Pow(PND, 2.14);
		val = Math.Max(0.01, Math.Min(0.09, val));
		double num2 = 1.405;
		double num3 = 0.03;
		if (val <= num3)
		{
			return num2 + 0.405 * Math.Tanh((val - num3) / 0.01);
		}
		return num2 + 0.4125 * (1.0 - Math.Exp((num3 - val) / 0.015));
	}

	internal double GetHoldUpPsiParameter(double PNGV, double PNLV, double PNLE, double PND, double P)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		double viscosityCoefficient = GetViscosityCoefficient(PNLE);
		double x = P / 101325.0;
		double d = viscosityCoefficient * PNLV * Math.Pow(x, 0.1) / (PND * Math.Pow(PNGV, 0.575));
		d = Math.Log10(d);
		double num = -2.08;
		double num2 = -4.0;
		double num3 = -4.7;
		double num4 = -5.0;
		double num5 = 0.14 * Math.Pow(10.0, (d - num4) / 1.6);
		double num6 = Math.Exp(-0.19 * (d - num) * (d - num)) - 0.17 * Math.Exp(-1.5 * (d - num2) * (d - num2));
		double num7 = 1.0 - 0.17 * Math.Exp(-1.5 * (num - num2) * (num - num2));
		num6 /= num7;
		if (d < num4)
		{
			result = num5;
		}
		else if (d >= num4 && d < num3)
		{
			double num8 = (d - num4) / (num3 - num4);
			result = (1.0 - num8) * num5 + num8 * num6;
		}
		else if (d >= num3)
		{
			result = num6;
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class HagedornBrownPressureGradientCorrelation : PipeFlowCorrelation, IPressureGradientCorrelation
{
	public PressureGradientMethodEnum PressureGradientMethod => PressureGradientMethodEnum.HagedornAndBrown;

	public HagedornBrownPressureGradientCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public HagedornBrownPressureGradientCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.FlowStream == null || base.Diameter == 0.0)
		{
			return result;
		}
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (flowRegCorr != null)
		{
			pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		double num = CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		double num3 = CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		return num + num2 + num3;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (flowRegCorr != null)
		{
			pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		return CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.FlowStream == null || base.Diameter == 0.0)
		{
			return result;
		}
		if (base.FlowStream.NumberOfPhases == 1)
		{
			result = PipeFlowFunctions.FrictionPressureGradientOfSinglePhaseFlow(base.FlowStream, base.Diameter, base.Roughness, base.FrictionFactorMethod);
			mUsedReynoldsNum = GasReynoldsNumber() + LiquidReynoldsNumber(combinedPhase: true);
			mUsedFrictionFactor = FrictionFactor(mUsedReynoldsNum, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
		}
		else
		{
			IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
			double value = combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			double value2 = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double num = LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true);
			double num2 = GasSupVelocity();
			double num3 = num + num2;
			double re = value2 * num3 * base.Diameter / value;
			double num4 = FrictionFactor(re, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
			double num5 = CombinedLiquidHomogeneousDensity();
			double num6 = liqHoldCorr.CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
			result = -2.0 * num4 * num5 * num3 * num3 / base.Diameter * (num5 / num6);
			mUsedReynoldsNum = re;
			mUsedFrictionFactor = num4;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public bool AccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return false;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (flowRegCorr != null)
		{
			pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		return CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (liqHoldCorr != null)
		{
			double num = liqHoldCorr.CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
			result = (0.0 - num) * 9.80665 * Math.Sin(HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.InclinedAngle));
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		return 0.0;
	}
}
public class ProfesHTCCorrelation : IHeatTransferCoeffCorrelation
{
	private readonly IFlowStream mStream;

	private readonly double mDiameter;

	private readonly double mAngle;

	private readonly double mRoughness;

	private readonly double mHoldup;

	private readonly double mTwall;

	private readonly double mTx;

	public ProfesHTCCorrelation(IFlowStream strm, double diam, double angle, double roughness, double holdup, double Twall, double Tx)
	{
		mStream = strm;
		mDiameter = diam;
		mAngle = angle;
		mRoughness = roughness;
		mHoldup = holdup;
		mTwall = Twall;
		mTx = Tx;
	}

	public double CalculateHTC()
	{
		double sinth = Math.Sin(mAngle * Math.PI / 180.0);
		double value = mStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
		double value2 = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value;
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 0.0;
		double value3 = mStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
		double num3 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double vaptc = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double vapcp = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double vapvisc = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num4 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double liqtc = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double liqcp = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double liqvisc = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double st = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num5 = mHoldup;
		IPhase phaseByType = mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			num3 = phaseByType.Density.Value;
			vaptc = phaseByType.Conductivity.Value;
			vapcp = phaseByType.Cp.Value;
			vapvisc = phaseByType.Viscosity.Value;
			num = value2 * phaseByType.MassFraction.Value;
		}
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			num4 = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			liqtc = combinedLiquidPhase.Conductivity.Value;
			liqcp = combinedLiquidPhase.Cp.Value;
			liqvisc = combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			st = combinedLiquidPhase.SurfaceTension.Value;
			num2 = value2 * combinedLiquidPhase.MassFraction.Value;
		}
		if (num5 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			num5 = num2 / (num * (num4 / num3) + num2);
		}
		return ProfesHTCoeffCalc(mDiameter, mRoughness, sinth, value, mTwall, mTx, value2, num, num2, value3, num5, num3, vaptc, vapcp, vapvisc, num4, liqtc, liqcp, liqvisc, st);
	}

	private static double ProfesHTCoeffVap(double hdavg, double pcv, double pcpv, double prov, double pvisv, double pvv, double pfric, double prn, double ptw, double ptv)
	{
		double num = prov * pvv * hdavg / pvisv;
		double num2 = pcpv * pvisv / pcv;
		double num3 = pcv / hdavg;
		double num4 = 0.0;
		if (num >= 5000.0)
		{
			double num5 = 15.0 / num;
			double num6 = Math.Log10(num5) / Math.Log10(num5 + pfric / 3.715);
			num6 *= num6;
			num4 = 0.023 * Math.Pow(num, 0.8) * Math.Pow(num2, prn) * num6 * num3;
		}
		else if (num >= 2000.0)
		{
			double num7 = 0.003;
			double num8 = Math.Log10(num7) / Math.Log10(num7 + pfric / 3.715);
			num8 *= num8;
			double num9 = 0.023 * Math.Pow(5000.0, 0.8) * Math.Pow(num2, prn) * num8 * num3;
			num4 = num9 / 3000.0 * (num - 2000.0);
		}
		double num10 = 3.66 * num3;
		double num11 = num2 * prov * prov * 9.80665 * Math.Abs(ptw - ptv);
		double num12 = pvisv * pvisv * (ptv + 273.15);
		double num13 = 0.13 * pcv * Math.Pow(num11 / num12, 0.333);
		double num14 = ((num4 > num10) ? num4 : num10);
		return (num14 > num13) ? num14 : num13;
	}

	private static double ProfesHTCoeffLiq(double hdavg, double pcl, double pcpl, double prol, double pvisl, double pvl, double prn, double pfric)
	{
		hdavg = ((hdavg < 0.0) ? 0.0 : hdavg);
		double num = prol * pvl * hdavg / pvisl;
		double x = pcpl * pvisl / pcl;
		double num2 = pcl / hdavg;
		double num3 = 0.0;
		if (num >= 5000.0)
		{
			double num4 = 15.0 / num;
			double num5 = Math.Log10(num4) / Math.Log10(num4 + pfric / 3.715);
			num5 *= num5;
			num3 = 0.023 * Math.Pow(num, 0.8) * Math.Pow(x, prn) * num5 * num2;
		}
		else if (num >= 2000.0)
		{
			double num6 = 0.003;
			double num7 = Math.Log10(num6) / Math.Log10(num6 + pfric / 3.715);
			num7 *= num7;
			double num8 = 0.023 * Math.Pow(5000.0, 0.8) * Math.Pow(x, prn) * num7 * num2;
			num3 = num8 / 3000.0 * (num - 2000.0);
		}
		double num9 = 3.66 * num2;
		if (!(num3 > num9))
		{
			return num9;
		}
		return num3;
	}

	private static double ProfesHTCoeffCalc(double diam, double roughness, double sinth, double temp, double twall, double tamb, double massflow, double vapflow, double liqflow, double mixdens, double hliq, double vapdens, double vaptc, double vapcp, double vapvisc, double liqdens, double liqtc, double liqcp, double liqvisc, double st)
	{
		double num = 1.0 - hliq;
		double prn = ((temp > tamb) ? 0.3 : 0.4);
		double pfric = roughness / diam;
		double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * diam * diam;
		if (num > 0.98)
		{
			double pvv = massflow / (vapdens * num2);
			return ProfesHTCoeffVap(diam, vaptc, vapcp, vapdens, vapvisc, pvv, pfric, prn, twall, temp);
		}
		if (num < 0.001)
		{
			double pvl = massflow / (liqdens * num2);
			return ProfesHTCoeffLiq(diam, liqtc, liqcp, liqdens, liqvisc, pvl, prn, pfric);
		}
		double num3 = 0.0;
		double num4 = 0.0;
		double num5 = 0.0;
		double num6 = liqflow / (liqdens * num2 * (1.0 - num));
		double num7 = vapflow / (vapdens * num2 * num);
		ProfesHTMap(num, num6, num7, sinth, diam, num2, vapdens, liqdens, out var regimes, out var fcav, out var wfhf);
		if (regimes[0])
		{
			double pvl2 = massflow / (mixdens * num2);
			double num8 = num7 / num6;
			double num9 = 1.0 / (1.0 + (1.0 - num) / num * liqdens / (vapdens * num8));
			double pvisl = 1.0 / (num9 / vapvisc + (1.0 - num9) / liqvisc);
			return ProfesHTCoeffLiq(diam, liqtc, liqcp, mixdens, pvisl, pvl2, prn, pfric);
		}
		if (regimes[1])
		{
			double num10 = (1.0 - 2.37341 * num + 9.935722 * num * num - 26.0088 * Math.Pow(num, 3.0) + 29.07746 * Math.Pow(num, 4.0) - 11.631 * Math.Pow(num, 5.0)) * diam;
			double num11 = num10 * 2.0 / diam - 1.0;
			double num12 = diam * Math.Sqrt(1.0 - num11 * num11);
			double num13 = diam * (Math.PI - Math.Acos(num11));
			double num14 = Math.PI * diam - num13;
			double num15 = num2 * (1.0 - num);
			double num16 = num * num2;
			double hdavg = 4.0 * num16 / (num12 + num14);
			double hdavg2 = 4.0 * num15 / num13;
			double num17 = ProfesHTCoeffLiq(hdavg2, liqtc, liqcp, liqdens, liqvisc, num6, prn, pfric);
			num3 = num17 * (1.0 - num);
			double num18 = ProfesHTCoeffVap(hdavg, vaptc, vapcp, vapdens, vapvisc, num7, pfric, prn, twall, temp);
			num3 = (num17 * num13 + num18 * num14) / (Math.PI * diam);
		}
		if (regimes[2])
		{
			double pvl3 = num7 * num + num6 * (1.0 - num);
			double num19 = ProfesHTCoeffLiq(diam, liqtc, liqcp, liqdens, liqvisc, pvl3, prn, pfric);
			num4 = num19 * (1.0 - num);
			double num20 = ProfesHTCoeffVap(diam, vaptc, vapcp, vapdens, vapvisc, num7, pfric, prn, twall, temp);
			num4 = num19 * (1.0 - num) + num20 * num;
		}
		if (regimes[3])
		{
			double num21 = ((liqdens > vapdens) ? (liqdens - vapdens) : 0.0);
			double num22 = 2.33 * Math.Sqrt(Math.Sqrt(num21 * 4.0 * st) / vapdens);
			double num23 = 0.0;
			if (num6 > num22)
			{
				num23 = 1.0 - Math.Exp(-1.0 * (num6 - num22) * 0.23);
			}
			num23 = ((num23 > 0.0001) ? num23 : 0.0001);
			num23 = ((num23 > 1.0) ? 1.0 : num23);
			double num24 = ((!(num6 > 1E-09)) ? ((1.0 - num) * (1.0 - num23)) : ((1.0 - num) * (1.0 - num6 / num7 * num23)));
			double num25 = 0.5 * diam * Math.Sqrt(1.0 - num24);
			double num26 = 0.5 * diam - num25;
			double pvl4 = num6 * (1.0 - num23);
			num5 = ProfesHTCoeffLiq(2.0 * num26, liqtc, liqcp, liqdens, liqvisc, pvl4, prn, pfric);
		}
		return wfhf * num3 + (1.0 - wfhf) * ((1.0 - fcav) * num4 + fcav * num5);
	}

	private static void ProfesHTMap(double alp, double liqvel, double vapvel, double sinth, double diam, double area, double vapdens, double liqdens, out bool[] regimes, out double fcav, out double wfhf)
	{
		fcav = 0.0;
		wfhf = 0.0;
		regimes = new bool[4];
		if (Math.Abs(sinth) < 0.174)
		{
			double num = ((!(alp > 0.999) && !(alp < 0.001)) ? ((1.0 - 2.37341 * alp + 9.935722 * alp * alp - 26.0088 * Math.Pow(alp, 3.0) + 29.07746 * Math.Pow(alp, 4.0) - 11.631 * Math.Pow(alp, 5.0)) * diam) : ((1.0 - alp) * diam));
			if (num > 0.001 * diam && num < 0.9999 * diam)
			{
				double num2 = Math.Sqrt(diam * diam - Math.Pow(2.0 * num - diam, 2.0));
				double num3 = Math.Sqrt(1.0 - sinth * sinth);
				double num4 = liqdens - vapdens;
				num4 = ((num4 > 0.001) ? num4 : 0.001);
				double num5 = (1.0 - num / diam) * Math.Sqrt(num4 * 9.80665 * alp * area / (vapdens * num2));
				if (vapvel < 2.0 * num5)
				{
					regimes[1] = true;
					num5 = ((num5 > 0.01) ? num5 : 0.01);
					double num6 = 2.0 - vapvel / num5;
					num6 = ((num6 < 1.0) ? num6 : 1.0);
					wfhf = (3.0 - 2.0 * num6) * num6 * num6;
				}
			}
			double num7 = ((alp < 0.6) ? alp : 0.6);
			num7 = ((num7 > 0.4) ? num7 : 0.4);
			fcav = Math.Pow(5.0 * num7 - 2.0, 2.0) * (7.0 - 10.0 * num7);
			if (alp < 0.6 && wfhf < 1.0)
			{
				regimes[2] = true;
			}
			if (alp > 0.4 && wfhf < 1.0)
			{
				regimes[3] = true;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if (alp < 0.75 && alp > 0.5)
			{
				fcav = Math.Pow(4.0 * alp - 2.0, 2.0) * (7.0 - 8.0 * alp);
			}
			else if (alp >= 0.75)
			{
				fcav = 1.0;
			}
			if (alp < 0.25)
			{
				regimes[0] = true;
			}
			if (alp >= 0.25 && alp < 0.75)
			{
				regimes[2] = true;
			}
			if (alp > 0.5)
			{
				regimes[3] = true;
			}
		}
	}
}
public class BloHTCCorrelation : IHeatTransferCoeffCorrelation
{
	private readonly IFlowStream mStream;

	private readonly double mDiameter;

	private readonly double mLength;

	public BloHTCCorrelation(IFlowStream strm, double diam, double length)
	{
		mStream = strm;
		mDiameter = diam;
		mLength = length;
	}

	private static double BloHTC(double RHO, double U, double D, double VISC, double PRANTL, double GASCON, double AL, double ENTRYP)
	{
		double num = RHO * U * D / VISC;
		double num2 = Math.Max(D, AL);
		double num3;
		if (num < 0.0)
		{
			num3 = 3.66;
		}
		else if (num < 2100.0)
		{
			double num4 = num * PRANTL * D / num2;
			num3 = ((!(num4 < 16.6)) ? (1.86 * Math.Pow(num4, 0.333)) : (3.66 + 0.085 * num4 / (1.0 + 0.047 * Math.Pow(num4, 0.667))));
		}
		else if (num < 10000.0)
		{
			double num5 = 2100.0 * PRANTL * D / num2;
			double num6 = ((!(num5 < 16.6)) ? (1.86 * Math.Pow(num5, 0.333)) : (3.66 + 0.085 * num5 / (1.0 + 0.047 * Math.Pow(num5, 0.667))));
			double num7 = 36.45 * Math.Pow(PRANTL, 0.333) * (1.0 + ENTRYP * D / num2);
			num3 = num6 + (num7 - num6) * (num - 2100.0) / 7900.0;
		}
		else
		{
			num3 = 0.023 * Math.Pow(num, 0.8) * Math.Pow(PRANTL, 0.333) * (1.0 + ENTRYP * D / num2);
		}
		return num3 * GASCON / D;
	}

	public double CalculateHTC()
	{
		double value = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value;
		double num = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num2 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num3 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num4 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num5 = 0.0;
		double num6 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num7 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num8 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num9 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num10 = 0.0;
		IPhase phaseByType = mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			num = phaseByType.Density.Value;
			num2 = phaseByType.Conductivity.Value;
			num4 = phaseByType.Viscosity.Value;
			num3 = num4 * phaseByType.Cp.Value / num2;
			num5 = value * phaseByType.MassFraction.Value / num;
		}
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			num6 = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			num7 = combinedLiquidPhase.Conductivity.Value;
			num9 = combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			num8 = num9 * combinedLiquidPhase.Cp.Value / num7;
			num10 = value * combinedLiquidPhase.MassFraction.Value / num6;
		}
		double num11 = num5 / (num5 + num10);
		double num12 = num11 * num + (1.0 - num11) * num6;
		double u = value / num12 / (Math.PI * mDiameter * mDiameter / 4.0);
		double vISC = num11 * num4 + (1.0 - num11) * num9;
		double pRANTL = num11 * num3 + (1.0 - num11) * num8;
		double gASCON = num11 * num2 + (1.0 - num11) * num7;
		return BloHTC(num12, u, mDiameter, vISC, pRANTL, gASCON, mLength, 0.0);
	}
}
public class HTFSHTCCorrelation : IHeatTransferCoeffCorrelation
{
	private readonly IFlowStream mStream;

	private readonly double mDiameter;

	private readonly double mTwall;

	private readonly double mTx;

	public HTFSHTCCorrelation(IFlowStream strm, double diam, double Twall, double Tx)
	{
		mStream = strm;
		mDiameter = diam;
		mTwall = Twall;
		mTx = Tx;
	}

	public double CalculateHTC()
	{
		return Convection.HTCOfTwoPhaseInternalFlow(mStream, mDiameter, mTx, mTwall);
	}
}
public class HTFSPressureGradientCorrelation : PipeFlowCorrelation, IPressureGradientCorrelation
{
	public PressureGradientMethodEnum PressureGradientMethod => PressureGradientMethodEnum.HTFS;

	public HTFSPressureGradientCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public HTFSPressureGradientCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
	}

	public bool AccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return false;
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		double num = CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, flowRegCorr);
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, flowRegCorr);
		return num + num2;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (flowRegCorr != null)
		{
			pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		return CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (liqHoldCorr != null)
		{
			double num = liqHoldCorr.CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
			result = (0.0 - num) * 9.80665 * Math.Sin(HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.InclinedAngle));
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.FlowStream != null && base.Diameter > 0.0)
		{
			if (base.FlowStream.NumberOfPhases == 1)
			{
				result = PipeFlowFunctions.FrictionPressureGradientOfSinglePhaseFlow(base.FlowStream, base.Diameter, base.Roughness, base.FrictionFactorMethod);
				mUsedReynoldsNum = GasReynoldsNumber() + LiquidReynoldsNumber(combinedPhase: true);
				mUsedFrictionFactor = FrictionFactor(mUsedReynoldsNum, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
			}
			else if (base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
			{
				result = PipeFlowFunctions.FrictionPressureGradientOf2LiqPhaseFlow(base.FlowStream, base.Diameter, base.Roughness, base.FrictionFactorMethod);
				mUsedReynoldsNum = LiquidReynoldsNumber(combinedPhase: true);
				mUsedFrictionFactor = FrictionFactor(mUsedReynoldsNum, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
			}
			else
			{
				IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
				IPhase phaseByType = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
				double value = combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value;
				double value2 = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
				double value3 = phaseByType.Viscosity.Value;
				double value4 = phaseByType.Density.Value;
				double num = LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true);
				double num2 = GasSupVelocity();
				double num3 = LiquidFlux(combinedPhase: true) + GasFlux();
				double re = GasReynoldsNumber();
				double re2 = LiquidReynoldsNumber(combinedPhase: true);
				double num4 = FrictionFactor(re, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
				double num5 = FrictionFactor(re2, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
				double num6 = -2.0 * num4 * value4 * num2 * num2 / base.Diameter;
				double num7 = -2.0 * num5 * value2 * num * num / base.Diameter;
				double num8 = TwoPhaseFlow.Baroczy(value2, value4, value, value3);
				if (num8 < 0.00316)
				{
					num8 = 0.00316;
				}
				double x = ((!(num3 >= 300.0)) ? (300.0 + (300.0 - num3) * (300.0 - num3) / 40.0) : num3);
				double num9 = 2.0 + 32.0 * Math.Pow(1.0 - 0.16 * Math.Pow(2.5 + Math.Log10(num8), 2.0), 3.0) / (1.0 + 0.005664 * Math.Pow(x, 0.8));
				double num10 = Math.Sqrt(value2 / value4) + Math.Sqrt(value4 / value2);
				double num11;
				if (num9 > num10)
				{
					num11 = num9;
				}
				else
				{
					double d = CombinedLiquidHomogeneousDensity();
					double num12 = Math.Sqrt(d) * Math.Pow(value2, 0.125) / Math.Pow(value4, 0.625);
					num11 = ((num9 >= num12) ? num9 : ((!(num10 >= num12)) ? num10 : num12));
				}
				double num13 = 0.5 * num11 * (1.0 + Math.Pow(10.0, -200.0 * base.Roughness / base.Diameter));
				double d2 = num7 * num6;
				result = num7 + (0.0 - num13) * Math.Sqrt(d2) + num6;
				mUsedReynoldsNum = liqHoldCorr.CalculateOverallDensity(pattern) * (num + num2) * base.Diameter / CombinedLiquidHomogeneousViscosity();
				mUsedFrictionFactor = FrictionFactor(mUsedReynoldsNum, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		return 0.0;
	}
}
public class HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation : PipeFlowCorrelation, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation
{
	public enum FittingType
	{
		Pipe,
		Contraction,
		Expansion,
		Orifice,
		Valve
	}

	private FittingType mFittingType;

	public LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum LiquidHoldUpMethod => LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum.HTFS;

	public HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation()
	{
		mFittingType = FittingType.Pipe;
	}

	public HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
		mFittingType = FittingType.Pipe;
	}

	public HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness, FittingType tfittingtype)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
		mFittingType = tfittingtype;
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateLiquidHoldUp(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		IPhase phaseByType = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		double result = 0.0;
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null && phaseByType != null)
		{
			double area = HTFSUtility.FlowArea(base.Diameter);
			double massFlux = HTFSUtility.CalculateMassFlux(base.FlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value, area);
			double num = TwoPhaseFlow.VoidFractionInPipe(combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value, phaseByType.Density.Value, combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value, phaseByType.Viscosity.Value, phaseByType.MassFraction.Value, massFlux, base.InclinedAngle);
			result = 1.0 - num;
		}
		else if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			result = 1.0;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateWaterHoldUp()
	{
		return RealProperty.EMPTY;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double liqHoldup = CalculateLiquidHoldUp(pattern);
		return CombinedLiquidHoldupDensity(liqHoldup);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double num = CalculateLiquidHoldUp(pattern);
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (num == 1.0)
		{
			result = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
		}
		else if (num == 0.0)
		{
			result = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Density.Value;
		}
		else
		{
			IPhase phaseByType = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
			IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
			if (mFittingType == FittingType.Pipe || mFittingType == FittingType.Orifice || mFittingType == FittingType.Valve)
			{
				result = 1.0 / TwoPhaseFlow.EquivSpecVolume(combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value, phaseByType.Density.Value, phaseByType.MassFraction.Value);
			}
			else if (mFittingType == FittingType.Contraction)
			{
				double value = phaseByType.MassFraction.Value;
				double value2 = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
				double value3 = phaseByType.Density.Value;
				double num2 = TwoPhaseFlow.MultiplierOfMartinelliAtL0(value2, value3, value);
				result = value2 / num2;
			}
			else if (mFittingType == FittingType.Expansion)
			{
				double value4 = phaseByType.MassFraction.Value;
				double value5 = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
				double value6 = phaseByType.Density.Value;
				double num3 = value4 * value4 / (1.0 - num) / value6 + (1.0 - value4) * (1.0 - value4) / num / value5;
				result = 1.0 / num3;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class HTFSCFittingLiquidHoldUpCorrelation : PipeFlowCorrelation, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation
{
	public enum FittingType
	{
		User,
		SuddenContraction,
		SuddenExpansion,
		ThinPlate,
		ThickPlate,
		GateValve,
		GlobeValve
	}

	private FittingType mFittingType;

	private double mCstar;

	public FittingType TypeOfFitting
	{
		get
		{
			return mFittingType;
		}
		set
		{
			mFittingType = value;
		}
	}

	public double UserCoefficient
	{
		set
		{
			mCstar = value;
		}
	}

	public LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum LiquidHoldUpMethod => LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum.HTFS;

	public HTFSCFittingLiquidHoldUpCorrelation()
	{
		mFittingType = FittingType.User;
		mCstar = 1.0;
	}

	public HTFSCFittingLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness, FittingType tfittingtype)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
		mFittingType = tfittingtype;
		mCstar = 1.0;
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateLiquidHoldUp(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		return CombinedLiquidNoSlipHoldUp();
	}

	public double CalculateWaterHoldUp()
	{
		return RealProperty.EMPTY;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		return CombinedLiquidHomogeneousDensity();
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		IPhase phaseByType = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		if (phaseByType != null && combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			double value = phaseByType.Density.Value;
			double value2 = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double value3 = phaseByType.Viscosity.Value;
			double value4 = combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			double massFlux = LiquidFlux(combinedPhase: true) + GasFlux();
			double value5 = phaseByType.MassFraction.Value;
			double num = 0.0;
			if (TypeOfFitting == FittingType.User)
			{
				double num2 = Math.Sqrt(value2 / value) + Math.Sqrt(value / value2);
				num = mCstar * num2;
			}
			else
			{
				num = TwoPhaseFlow.CoefficeCByFittingsName(GetFittingNameString(mFittingType), value2, value, value4, value3, value5, massFlux);
			}
			double num3 = (1.0 - value5) * (1.0 - value5) / value2 + value5 * value5 / value + num * (1.0 - value5) * value5 / Math.Sqrt(value * value2);
			result = 1.0 / num3;
		}
		else if (phaseByType == null && combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			result = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
		}
		else if (phaseByType != null && combinedLiquidPhase == null)
		{
			result = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Density.Value;
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal string GetFittingNameString(FittingType tFitting)
	{
		string result = "";
		switch (tFitting)
		{
		case FittingType.SuddenContraction:
			result = "SuddenContraction";
			break;
		case FittingType.SuddenExpansion:
			result = "SuddenExpansion";
			break;
		case FittingType.ThinPlate:
			result = "ThinPlate";
			break;
		case FittingType.ThickPlate:
			result = "ThickPlate";
			break;
		case FittingType.GateValve:
			result = "GateValve";
			break;
		case FittingType.GlobeValve:
			result = "GlobeValve";
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class LockhartMartinelliPressureGradientCorrelation : PipeFlowCorrelation, IPressureGradientCorrelation
{
	public PressureGradientMethodEnum PressureGradientMethod => PressureGradientMethodEnum.LockhartMartinelli;

	public LockhartMartinelliPressureGradientCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public LockhartMartinelliPressureGradientCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.FlowStream == null || base.Diameter == 0.0)
		{
			return result;
		}
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (flowRegCorr != null)
		{
			pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		double num = CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		return num + num2;
	}

	public bool AccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return false;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.FlowStream == null || base.Diameter == 0.0)
		{
			return result;
		}
		if (base.FlowStream.NumberOfPhases == 1)
		{
			result = PipeFlowFunctions.FrictionPressureGradientOfSinglePhaseFlow(base.FlowStream, base.Diameter, base.Roughness, base.FrictionFactorMethod);
			mUsedReynoldsNum = GasReynoldsNumber() + LiquidReynoldsNumber(combinedPhase: true);
			mUsedFrictionFactor = FrictionFactor(mUsedReynoldsNum, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
		}
		else if (base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
		{
			result = PipeFlowFunctions.FrictionPressureGradientOf2LiqPhaseFlow(base.FlowStream, base.Diameter, base.Roughness, base.FrictionFactorMethod);
			mUsedReynoldsNum = LiquidReynoldsNumber(combinedPhase: true);
			mUsedFrictionFactor = FrictionFactor(mUsedReynoldsNum, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
		}
		else
		{
			double num = LiquidFlux(combinedPhase: true);
			double re = LiquidReynoldsNumber(combinedPhase: true);
			double num2 = FrictionFactor(re, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
			double value = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			result = 2.0 * num2 * num * num / (value * base.Diameter);
			double value2 = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Density.Value;
			double value3 = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).MassFraction.Value;
			double num3 = TwoPhaseFlow.MultiplierOfMartinelliAtL0(value, value2, value3);
			result = (0.0 - result) * num3;
			mUsedReynoldsNum = re;
			mUsedFrictionFactor = num2;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (flowRegCorr != null)
		{
			pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		return CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (liqHoldCorr != null)
		{
			double num = liqHoldCorr.CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
			result = (0.0 - num) * 9.80665 * Math.Sin(HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.InclinedAngle));
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		return 0.0;
	}
}
public class MartinelliLiquidHoldUpCorrelation : PipeFlowCorrelation, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation
{
	public LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum LiquidHoldUpMethod => LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum.Martinelli;

	public MartinelliLiquidHoldUpCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public MartinelliLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateLiquidHoldUp(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		IPhase phaseByType = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		double result = 0.0;
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null && phaseByType != null)
		{
			double value = phaseByType.MassFraction.Value;
			double value2 = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double value3 = phaseByType.Density.Value;
			double num = TwoPhaseFlow.MultiplierOfMartinelliAtL0(value2, value3, value);
			result = (value2 / num - value3) / (value2 - value3);
		}
		else if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			result = 1.0;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateWaterHoldUp()
	{
		return RealProperty.EMPTY;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		IPhase phaseByType = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null && phaseByType != null)
		{
			double value = phaseByType.MassFraction.Value;
			double value2 = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double value3 = phaseByType.Density.Value;
			double num = TwoPhaseFlow.MultiplierOfMartinelliAtL0(value2, value3, value);
			result = value2 / num;
		}
		else if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			result = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
		}
		else if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			result = phaseByType.Density.Value;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		return CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		return CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
	}
}
public abstract class OrificeFrictionFactorCorrelation : ICorrelationManager, IFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private IFlowStream mStream;

	private double mDiameter;

	private double mThickness;

	private double mThroat;

	protected ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation;

	public double Diameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mDiameter;
		}
		set
		{
			mDiameter = value;
		}
	}

	public double PlateThickness
	{
		get
		{
			return mThickness;
		}
		set
		{
			mThickness = value;
		}
	}

	public double ThroatDiameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mThroat;
		}
		set
		{
			mThroat = value;
		}
	}

	public ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation LiquidHoldUpCorrelation
	{
		get
		{
			return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation;
		}
		set
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = value;
		}
	}

	public IFlowStream FlowStream
	{
		get
		{
			return mStream;
		}
		set
		{
			mStream = value;
			if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation is PipeFlowCorrelation)
			{
				(mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation as PipeFlowCorrelation).FlowStream = mStream;
			}
		}
	}

	public OrificeFrictionFactorCorrelation()
	{
		mStream = null;
		mDiameter = 0.0;
		mThickness = 0.0;
		mThroat = 0.0;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = null;
	}

	public OrificeFrictionFactorCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double plateThickness)
	{
		mStream = inStream;
		mDiameter = mainDiam;
		mThickness = plateThickness;
		mThroat = throatDiam;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = null;
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient()
	{
		double num = CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
		double num3 = CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
		return num + num2 + num3;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (mStream != null && mDiameter > 0.0)
		{
			double num = CalculateKFactor();
			double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(mDiameter, 2.0);
			double num3 = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num2;
			result = -0.5 * num * num3 * num3 / CalculateEquivalentDensity();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity()
	{
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation == null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		}
		return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity()
	{
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation == null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		}
		return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public bool PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return false;
	}

	public abstract double CalculateKFactor();
}
public class OrificeCraneFrictionCorrelation : OrificeFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	public OrificeCraneFrictionCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public OrificeCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double plateThickness)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, plateThickness)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, mainDiam, 0.0, 0.0);
	}

	public OrificeCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double plateThickness, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, plateThickness)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.Diameter > 0.0)
		{
			result = Crane.KFactorForOrifice(base.Diameter, base.ThroatDiameter, base.PlateThickness);
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class OrificeHTFSFrictionCorrelation : OrificeFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	public OrificeHTFSFrictionCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public OrificeHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double plateThickness)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, plateThickness)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, mainDiam, 0.0, 0.0, HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.FittingType.Orifice);
	}

	public OrificeHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double plateThickness, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, plateThickness)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.Diameter > 0.0 && base.FlowStream != null)
		{
			double viscosity = ((base.FlowStream.NumberOfPhases >= 2) ? base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value : base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Viscosity.Value);
			double num = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(base.Diameter, 2.0);
			double massFlux = base.FlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num;
			result = SinglePhaseFlow.KFactorForOrifice(base.Diameter, base.ThroatDiameter, base.PlateThickness, massFlux, viscosity);
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class ORKFlowRegimeCorrelation : PipeFlowCorrelation, IFlowRegimeCorrelation
{
	public FlowRegimeMethodEnum FlowRegimeMethod => FlowRegimeMethodEnum.Orkiszewski;

	public ORKFlowRegimeCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public ORKFlowRegimeCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
	}

	public FlowRegimeEnum EstimateFlowPattern()
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum result = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (base.FlowStream == null || base.Diameter == 0.0)
		{
			return result;
		}
		if (base.FlowStream.NumberOfPhases < 2)
		{
			result = ((base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType != PhaseType.Vapour) ? FlowRegimeEnum.Liquid : FlowRegimeEnum.Vapour);
		}
		else if (base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
		{
			result = FlowRegimeEnum.Liquid;
		}
		else
		{
			IPhase phaseByType = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
			double num = LiquidViscosityNumber(combinedPhase: true);
			double num2 = LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true);
			double num3 = GasSupVelocity();
			double num4 = num2 + num3;
			double num5 = LiquidVelocityNumber();
			double num6 = GasVelocityNumber();
			double num7 = PipeDiameterNumber(combinedPhase: true);
			double num8 = FroudeNumber();
			double num9 = Math.Max(0.13, 1.071 - 7.139 * num8);
			double num10 = 50.0 + 36.0 * num5;
			double num11 = 75.0 + 84.0 * Math.Pow(num5, 0.75);
			if (phaseByType.MassFraction.Value < num9)
			{
				result = FlowRegimeEnum.Bubble;
			}
			else if (phaseByType.MassFraction.Value >= num9 && num6 <= num10)
			{
				result = FlowRegimeEnum.Slug;
			}
			else if (num6 > num10 && num6 <= num11)
			{
				result = FlowRegimeEnum.Transition;
			}
			else if (num6 > num11)
			{
				result = FlowRegimeEnum.Mist;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public FlowRegimeEnum EstimateWaterFlowPattern()
	{
		return FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
	}
}
public class ORKLiquidHoldUpCorrelation : PipeFlowCorrelation, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation
{
	public LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum LiquidHoldUpMethod => LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum.Orkiszewski;

	public ORKLiquidHoldUpCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public ORKLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		return CalculateLiquidHoldUp(pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (base.FlowStream == null || base.Diameter == 0.0)
		{
			return result;
		}
		switch (pattern)
		{
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Vapour:
			result = 0.0;
			break;
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Liquid:
			result = 1.0;
			break;
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Bubble:
		{
			double num12 = 0.24;
			double num13 = 1.0 + (LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true) + GasSupVelocity()) / num12;
			double num14 = num13 * num13 - 4.0 * GasSupVelocity() / num12;
			if (num14 > 0.0)
			{
				result = 1.0 - 0.5 * (num13 - Math.Sqrt(num14));
			}
			break;
		}
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Slug:
		{
			IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
			IPhase phaseByType = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
			double value = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double value2 = phaseByType.Density.Value;
			double value3 = combinedLiquidPhase.SurfaceTension.Value;
			double value4 = combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			double num = LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true) + GasSupVelocity();
			double reM = value * base.Diameter * num / value4;
			double num2 = 3.05;
			double num3 = Math.Log10(1000.0 * value4 + 1.0);
			double slipVelocity = GetSlipVelocity(base.Diameter, value, value4, reM);
			double num4 = 0.0;
			double num5 = 0.0;
			if (num < num2)
			{
				num4 = 0.00236 * num3 / Math.Pow(base.Diameter, 1.415) - 0.14 + 0.167 * Math.Log10(num) + 0.113 * Math.Log10(base.Diameter);
				num4 = Math.Max(num4, -0.213 * num);
				double num6 = base.FlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value / (Math.PI / 4.0 * base.Diameter * base.Diameter);
				num5 = (num6 + value * slipVelocity) / (num + slipVelocity) + value * num4;
			}
			else
			{
				double num7 = 1.0 / Math.Pow(base.Diameter, 1.571);
				double num8 = 0.00537 / Math.Pow(base.Diameter, 1.371) - 0.000798 * num7;
				num4 = num3 * num8 + 0.0824 + 0.244 * Math.Log10(base.Diameter) - Math.Log10(num) * (0.00155 * num3 * num7 + 0.722 + 0.63 * Math.Log10(base.Diameter));
				int num9 = 0;
				double num10 = base.FlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value / (Math.PI / 4.0 * base.Diameter * base.Diameter);
				num5 = (num10 + value * slipVelocity) / (num + slipVelocity) + value * num4;
				double num11 = (0.0 - slipVelocity) * (1.0 - num5 / value) / (num + slipVelocity);
				while (num4 < num11 && num9 <= 3)
				{
					num4 = num11;
					num5 = (num10 + value * slipVelocity) / (num + slipVelocity) + value * num4;
					num11 = (0.0 - slipVelocity) * (1.0 - num5 / value) / (num + slipVelocity);
				}
			}
			result = 1.0 - (value - num5) / (value - value2);
			break;
		}
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Mist:
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Transition:
		{
			DunsAndRosLiquidHoldUpCorrelation dunsAndRosLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new DunsAndRosLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(base.FlowStream, base.Diameter, base.InclinedAngle, base.Roughness);
			result = dunsAndRosLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateLiquidHoldUp(pattern);
			break;
		}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateWaterHoldUp()
	{
		return RealProperty.EMPTY;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		return CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double liqHoldup = CalculateLiquidHoldUp(pattern);
		return CombinedLiquidHoldupDensity(liqHoldup);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		return CalculateEquivalentDensity(pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		return CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
	}

	internal double GetSlipVelocity(double diam, double denL, double visL, double ReM)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		int num2 = 10;
		double num3 = 8.74E-06 * ReM;
		double num4 = 3000.0;
		double num5 = 8000.0;
		double num6 = 0.001;
		double num7 = denL * diam / visL;
		double num8 = Math.Sqrt(9.80665 * diam);
		double num9 = -100.0;
		num = 0.5 * num8;
		bool flag = false;
		int num10 = 0;
		while (!flag && num10 < num2)
		{
			double num11 = num;
			double num12 = num7 * num11;
			double num13 = 0.35 * (1.0 - Math.Exp(-0.164 * num12));
			if (num12 <= num4)
			{
				num = (0.546 + num3) * num8;
			}
			else if (num12 > num4 && num12 <= num5)
			{
				int num14 = (int)Math.Truncate(num12 / 1000.0);
				double num15 = num12 / 1000.0 - (double)num14;
				if (num14 == 3 && ReM <= 3000.0)
				{
					double num16 = 1.0 + ReM * (0.000259 - ReM * 3.1E-08);
					double num17 = 1.0 + ReM * (9.03E-05 - ReM * 1.62E-09);
					num9 = (1.0 - num15) * num16 + num15 * num17;
				}
				else if (num14 == 4 && ReM <= 5000.0)
				{
					double num18 = 1.0 + ReM * (9.03E-05 - ReM * 1.62E-09);
					double num19 = 1.0 + ReM * (6.16E-05 - ReM * 2.1E-09);
					num9 = (1.0 - num15) * num18 + num15 * num19;
				}
				else if (num14 == 5 && ReM <= 6000.0)
				{
					double num20 = 1.0 + ReM * (6.16E-05 - ReM * 2.1E-09);
					double num21 = 1.0 + ReM * (4.42E-05 - ReM * 7.39E-09);
					num9 = (1.0 - num15) * num20 + num15 * num21;
				}
				else if (num14 == 6 && ReM <= 6000.0)
				{
					double num22 = 1.0 + ReM * (4.42E-05 - ReM * 7.39E-10);
					double num23 = 1.0 + ReM * (3.61E-05 - ReM * 6.37E-10);
					num9 = (1.0 - num15) * num22 + num15 * num23;
				}
				else if (num14 == 7 && ReM <= 6000.0)
				{
					double num24 = 1.0 + ReM * (3.61E-05 - ReM * 6.37E-10);
					double num25 = 1.0 + ReM * (3.34E-05 - ReM * 8.66E-10);
					num9 = (1.0 - num15) * num24 + num15 * num25;
				}
				if (num9 > 0.0)
				{
					num = num13 * num9 * num8;
				}
				else
				{
					double num26 = (0.251 + num3) * num8;
					num = 0.5 * num26 + Math.Sqrt(num26 * num26 + 11170.0 * visL / (denL * Math.Sqrt(diam)));
				}
			}
			else if (num12 > num5)
			{
				num = (0.35 + num3) * num8;
			}
			if (Math.Abs((num - num11) / num) < num6)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			num10++;
		}
		return num;
	}
}
public class ORKPressureGradientCorrelation : PipeFlowCorrelation, IPressureGradientCorrelation
{
	public PressureGradientMethodEnum PressureGradientMethod => PressureGradientMethodEnum.Orkiszewski;

	public ORKPressureGradientCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public ORKPressureGradientCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.FlowStream == null || base.Diameter == 0.0)
		{
			return result;
		}
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		double num = CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		double num3 = CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		return num + num2 + num3;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		return CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.FlowStream == null || base.Diameter == 0.0)
		{
			return result;
		}
		switch (pattern)
		{
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Vapour:
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Liquid:
			result = PipeFlowFunctions.FrictionPressureGradientOfSinglePhaseFlow(base.FlowStream, base.Diameter, base.Roughness, base.FrictionFactorMethod);
			mUsedReynoldsNum = GasReynoldsNumber() + LiquidReynoldsNumber(combinedPhase: true);
			mUsedFrictionFactor = FrictionFactor(mUsedReynoldsNum, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
			break;
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Bubble:
		{
			IPhase combinedLiquidPhase2 = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
			double value3 = combinedLiquidPhase2.Viscosity.Value;
			double value4 = combinedLiquidPhase2.Density.Value;
			double num6 = LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true);
			double num7 = GasSupVelocity();
			double num8 = num6 + num7;
			double num9 = liqHoldCorr.CalculateLiquidHoldUp(pattern);
			double re2 = LiquidReynoldsNumber(combinedPhase: true) / num9;
			double num10 = FrictionFactor(re2, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
			result = -2.0 * num10 * num6 * LiquidFlux(combinedPhase: true) / base.Diameter / num9 / num9;
			mUsedReynoldsNum = re2;
			mUsedFrictionFactor = num10;
			break;
		}
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Slug:
		{
			IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
			double value = combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			double value2 = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double num = LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true);
			double num2 = GasSupVelocity();
			double num3 = num + num2;
			double re = value2 * num3 * base.Diameter / value;
			double num4 = FrictionFactor(re, base.Roughness / base.Diameter);
			double num5 = liqHoldCorr.CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
			result = -2.0 * num4 * num5 * num3 * num3 / base.Diameter;
			mUsedReynoldsNum = re;
			mUsedFrictionFactor = num4;
			break;
		}
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Mist:
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Transition:
		{
			DunsAndRosPressureGradientCorrelation dunsAndRosPressureGradientCorrelation = new DunsAndRosPressureGradientCorrelation(base.FlowStream, base.Diameter, base.InclinedAngle, base.Roughness);
			dunsAndRosPressureGradientCorrelation.FrictionFactorMethod = base.FrictionFactorMethod;
			result = dunsAndRosPressureGradientCorrelation.CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
			mUsedReynoldsNum = dunsAndRosPressureGradientCorrelation.UsedReynoldsNumber;
			mUsedFrictionFactor = dunsAndRosPressureGradientCorrelation.UsedFrictionFactor;
			break;
		}
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal double GetAccelerationCorrection(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		switch (pattern)
		{
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Vapour:
		{
			IPhase phaseByID = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1);
			double num5 = GasSupVelocity();
			result = num5 * num5 * phaseByID.Density.Value / phaseByID.Pressure.Value;
			break;
		}
		case FlowRegimeEnum.Mist:
		{
			IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
			double num = CombinedLiquidHomogeneousDensity();
			double num2 = GasSupVelocity();
			double num3 = LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true);
			double num4 = num2 + num3;
			result = num4 * num2 * num / combinedLiquidPhase.Pressure.Value;
			break;
		}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public bool AccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return true;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		return CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double num = liqHoldCorr.CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
		return (0.0 - num) * 9.80665 * Math.Sin(HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.InclinedAngle));
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = flowRegCorr.EstimateFlowPattern();
		return CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.FlowStream != null && base.Diameter > 0.0)
		{
			double accelerationCorrection = GetAccelerationCorrection(pattern);
			double num = CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
			double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
			result = accelerationCorrection * (num + num2) / (1.0 - accelerationCorrection);
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public abstract class PipeFlowCorrelation
{
	private double mDiameter;

	private double mAngle;

	private double mRoughness;

	protected bool mUseAGAHead;

	private IFlowStream mStream;

	private FrictionFactorMethodEnum mFrictionFactorMethod;

	protected double mUsedFrictionFactor;

	protected double mUsedReynoldsNum;

	public double Diameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mDiameter;
		}
		set
		{
			mDiameter = value;
		}
	}

	public double InclinedAngle
	{
		get
		{
			return mAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public double Roughness
	{
		get
		{
			return mRoughness;
		}
		set
		{
			mRoughness = value;
		}
	}

	public double PipeLength
	{
		set
		{
		}
	}

	public bool UseAGAHead
	{
		set
		{
			mUseAGAHead = value;
		}
	}

	public IFlowStream FlowStream
	{
		get
		{
			return mStream;
		}
		set
		{
			mStream = value;
		}
	}

	public FrictionFactorMethodEnum FrictionFactorMethod
	{
		get
		{
			return mFrictionFactorMethod;
		}
		set
		{
			mFrictionFactorMethod = value;
		}
	}

	public double UsedFrictionFactor => mUsedFrictionFactor;

	public double UsedReynoldsNumber => mUsedReynoldsNum;

	public PipeFlowCorrelation()
	{
		mDiameter = 0.0;
		mAngle = 0.0;
		mRoughness = 0.0;
		mStream = null;
		mFrictionFactorMethod = FrictionFactorMethodEnum.Chen;
		mUsedFrictionFactor = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mUsedReynoldsNum = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mUseAGAHead = false;
	}

	public PipeFlowCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
	{
		mDiameter = diam;
		mAngle = angle;
		mRoughness = roughness;
		mStream = inStream;
		mFrictionFactorMethod = FrictionFactorMethodEnum.Chen;
		mUsedFrictionFactor = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mUsedReynoldsNum = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mUseAGAHead = false;
	}

	public double LiquidSupVelocity(bool combinedPhase)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase phase = null;
		phase = ((!combinedPhase) ? mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) : mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase);
		if (phase != null)
		{
			double num = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value * phase.MassFraction.Value / phase.Density.Value;
			result = num / (Math.PI * mDiameter * mDiameter * 0.25);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double GasSupVelocity()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase phaseByType = mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			double num = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value * phaseByType.MassFraction.Value / phaseByType.Density.Value;
			result = num / (Math.PI * mDiameter * mDiameter * 0.25);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double WaterSupVelocity()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase phaseByType = mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous);
		if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			double num = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value * phaseByType.MassFraction.Value / phaseByType.Density.Value;
			result = num / (Math.PI * mDiameter * mDiameter * 0.25);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double LiquidFlux(bool combinedPhase)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase phase = null;
		phase = ((!combinedPhase) ? mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) : mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase);
		if (phase != null)
		{
			double num = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value * phase.MassFraction.Value;
			result = num / (Math.PI * mDiameter * mDiameter * 0.25);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double GasFlux()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase phaseByType = mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			double num = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value * phaseByType.MassFraction.Value;
			result = num / (Math.PI * mDiameter * mDiameter * 0.25);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double LiquidVelocityNumber()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			double value = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double value2 = combinedLiquidPhase.SurfaceTension.Value;
			result = LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true) * Math.Pow(value / 9.80665 / value2, 0.25);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double GasVelocityNumber()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			double value = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double value2 = combinedLiquidPhase.SurfaceTension.Value;
			result = GasSupVelocity() * Math.Pow(value / 9.80665 / value2, 0.25);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double LiquidViscosityNumber(bool combinedPhase)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase phase = null;
		phase = ((!combinedPhase) ? mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) : mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase);
		if (phase != null)
		{
			double value = phase.Density.Value;
			double value2 = phase.SurfaceTension.Value;
			double value3 = phase.Viscosity.Value;
			result = value3 * Math.Pow(9.80665 / value / value2 / value2 / value2, 0.25);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double LiquidReynoldsNumber(bool combinedPhase)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase phase = null;
		phase = ((!combinedPhase) ? mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) : mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase);
		if (phase != null)
		{
			double value = phase.Density.Value;
			double value2 = phase.Viscosity.Value;
			result = value * LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase) * mDiameter / value2;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double GasReynoldsNumber()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase phaseByType = mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			double value = phaseByType.Density.Value;
			double value2 = phaseByType.Viscosity.Value;
			result = value * GasSupVelocity() * mDiameter / value2;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double PipeDiameterNumber(bool combinedPhase)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IPhase phase = null;
		phase = ((!combinedPhase) ? mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) : mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase);
		if (phase != null)
		{
			double value = phase.Density.Value;
			double value2 = phase.SurfaceTension.Value;
			result = mDiameter * Math.Sqrt(9.80665 * value / value2);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CombinedLiquidNoSlipHoldUp()
	{
		double num = GasSupVelocity();
		double num2 = LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true);
		return num2 / (num2 + num);
	}

	public double CombinedLiquidHoldupDensity(double liqHoldup)
	{
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		IPhase phaseByType = FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null && phaseByType != null)
		{
			result = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value * liqHoldup + phaseByType.Density.Value * (1.0 - liqHoldup);
		}
		else if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			result = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
		}
		else if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			result = phaseByType.Density.Value;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CombinedLiquidHomogeneousDensity()
	{
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		IPhase phaseByType = FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null && phaseByType != null)
		{
			double num = CombinedLiquidNoSlipHoldUp();
			result = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value * num + phaseByType.Density.Value * (1.0 - num);
		}
		else if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			result = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
		}
		else if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			result = phaseByType.Density.Value;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CombinedLiquidHomogeneousViscosity()
	{
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = mStream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		IPhase phaseByType = FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null && phaseByType != null)
		{
			double num = CombinedLiquidNoSlipHoldUp();
			result = combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value * num + phaseByType.Viscosity.Value * (1.0 - num);
		}
		else if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			result = combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value;
		}
		else if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			result = phaseByType.Viscosity.Value;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double FroudeNumber()
	{
		double num = GasSupVelocity() + LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: true);
		return num * num / 9.80665 / mDiameter;
	}

	public double FrictionFactor(double Re, double ed)
	{
		return PipeFlowFunctions.FrictionFactor(mFrictionFactorMethod, Re, ed);
	}
}
public class PipeCorrelationManager : ICorrelationManager
{
	private readonly IFlowRegimeCorrelation mFlowRegimeCorrelation;

	private readonly ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation;

	private readonly IPressureGradientCorrelation mPressureGradientCorrelation;

	private IFlowStream mStream;

	private double mDiameter;

	private double mAngle;

	private double mRoughness;

	private double mPipeLength;

	private double mPipeLengthMultiplier;

	private double mEquivPipeLength;

	private double mFittingRoughness;

	private double mFittingCoeffA;

	private double mFittingCoeffB;

	private bool mUseAGAHead;

	private FrictionFactorMethodEnum mFrictionFactorMethod;

	private double mUsedFrictionFactor;

	private double mUsedReynoldsNum;

	public IFlowRegimeCorrelation FlowRegimeCorrelation => mFlowRegimeCorrelation;

	public ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation LiquidHoldUpCorrelation => mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation;

	public IPressureGradientCorrelation PressureGradientCorrelation => mPressureGradientCorrelation;

	public IFlowStream FlowStream
	{
		set
		{
			mStream = value;
		}
	}

	public double Diameter
	{
		set
		{
			mDiameter = value;
		}
	}

	public double InclinedAngle
	{
		set
		{
			mAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public double Roughness
	{
		set
		{
			mRoughness = value;
		}
	}

	public double PipeLength
	{
		set
		{
			mPipeLength = value;
		}
	}

	public double PipeLengthMultiplier
	{
		set
		{
			mPipeLengthMultiplier = value;
		}
	}

	public double EquivalentPipeLength => mEquivPipeLength;

	public double UsedFrictionFactor => mUsedFrictionFactor;

	public double UsedReynoldsNumber => mUsedReynoldsNum;

	public FrictionFactorMethodEnum FrictionFactorMethod
	{
		set
		{
			mFrictionFactorMethod = value;
		}
	}

	public double FittingRoughness
	{
		set
		{
			mFittingRoughness = value;
		}
	}

	public double FittingCoeffA
	{
		set
		{
			mFittingCoeffA = value;
		}
	}

	public double FittingCoeffB
	{
		set
		{
			mFittingCoeffB = value;
		}
	}

	public bool UseAGAHead
	{
		set
		{
			mUseAGAHead = value;
		}
	}

	public PipeCorrelationManager()
	{
		mFlowRegimeCorrelation = null;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = null;
		mPressureGradientCorrelation = null;
		mStream = null;
		mDiameter = 0.0;
		mAngle = 0.0;
		mRoughness = 0.0;
		mFrictionFactorMethod = FrictionFactorMethodEnum.Chen;
		mUsedFrictionFactor = 0.0;
		mPipeLength = 0.0;
		mPipeLengthMultiplier = 1.0;
		mFittingRoughness = 0.0;
		mFittingCoeffA = 0.0;
		mFittingCoeffB = 0.0;
		mUsedReynoldsNum = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mUseAGAHead = false;
	}

	public PipeCorrelationManager(IPressureGradientCorrelation pgMethod, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation lhMethod, IFlowRegimeCorrelation frMethod, IFlowStream stream, double diam, double length, double angle, double roughness)
	{
		mStream = stream;
		mDiameter = diam;
		mAngle = angle;
		mRoughness = roughness;
		mPipeLength = length;
		mPressureGradientCorrelation = pgMethod;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = lhMethod;
		mFlowRegimeCorrelation = frMethod;
		mFrictionFactorMethod = FrictionFactorMethodEnum.Chen;
		mUsedFrictionFactor = 0.0;
		mPipeLengthMultiplier = 1.0;
		mFittingRoughness = 0.0;
		mFittingCoeffA = 0.0;
		mFittingCoeffB = 0.0;
		mUsedReynoldsNum = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mUseAGAHead = false;
	}

	internal void SetPipeDiameter(double diam)
	{
		if (mFlowRegimeCorrelation != null)
		{
			mFlowRegimeCorrelation.Diameter = diam;
		}
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation != null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.Diameter = diam;
		}
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation != null)
		{
			mPressureGradientCorrelation.Diameter = diam;
		}
	}

	internal void SetPipeRoughness(double roughness)
	{
		if (mFlowRegimeCorrelation != null)
		{
			mFlowRegimeCorrelation.Roughness = roughness;
		}
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation != null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.Roughness = roughness;
		}
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation != null)
		{
			mPressureGradientCorrelation.Roughness = roughness;
		}
	}

	internal void SetPipeAngle(double angle)
	{
		if (mFlowRegimeCorrelation != null)
		{
			mFlowRegimeCorrelation.InclinedAngle = angle;
		}
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation != null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.InclinedAngle = angle;
		}
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation != null)
		{
			mPressureGradientCorrelation.InclinedAngle = angle;
		}
	}

	internal void SetFlowstream(IFlowStream stream)
	{
		if (mFlowRegimeCorrelation != null)
		{
			mFlowRegimeCorrelation.FlowStream = stream;
		}
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation != null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.FlowStream = stream;
		}
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation != null)
		{
			mPressureGradientCorrelation.FlowStream = stream;
		}
	}

	internal void SetFrictionFactorMethod(FrictionFactorMethodEnum method)
	{
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation is PipeFlowCorrelation)
		{
			(mPressureGradientCorrelation as PipeFlowCorrelation).FrictionFactorMethod = method;
		}
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation is PipeFlowCorrelation)
		{
			(mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation as PipeFlowCorrelation).FrictionFactorMethod = method;
		}
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation is PipeFlowCorrelation)
		{
			(mPressureGradientCorrelation as PipeFlowCorrelation).FrictionFactorMethod = method;
		}
	}

	internal void SetPipeLength(double length)
	{
		if (mFlowRegimeCorrelation != null)
		{
			mFlowRegimeCorrelation.PipeLength = length;
		}
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation != null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.PipeLength = length;
		}
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation != null)
		{
			mPressureGradientCorrelation.PipeLength = length;
		}
	}

	internal void SetUseAGAHead(bool useAGAhead)
	{
		if (mFlowRegimeCorrelation is PipeFlowCorrelation)
		{
			(mFlowRegimeCorrelation as PipeFlowCorrelation).UseAGAHead = useAGAhead;
		}
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation is PipeFlowCorrelation)
		{
			(mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation as PipeFlowCorrelation).UseAGAHead = useAGAhead;
		}
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation is PipeFlowCorrelation)
		{
			(mPressureGradientCorrelation as PipeFlowCorrelation).UseAGAHead = useAGAhead;
		}
	}

	public void SetPipeParameters(double diam, double angle, double roughness)
	{
		mDiameter = diam;
		mAngle = angle;
		mRoughness = roughness;
	}

	internal void UpdateCorrelations()
	{
		SetFlowstream(mStream);
		SetPipeDiameter(mDiameter);
		SetPipeAngle(mAngle);
		SetPipeRoughness(mRoughness);
		SetFrictionFactorMethod(mFrictionFactorMethod);
		SetPipeLength(mPipeLength);
		SetUseAGAHead(mUseAGAHead);
	}

	public FlowRegimeEnum EstimateFlowPattern()
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum result = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (mFlowRegimeCorrelation != null)
		{
			UpdateCorrelations();
			result = mFlowRegimeCorrelation.EstimateFlowPattern();
		}
		else if (mStream != null && mStream.NumberOfPhases < 2)
		{
			result = ((mStream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType != PhaseType.Vapour) ? FlowRegimeEnum.Liquid : FlowRegimeEnum.Vapour);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public FlowRegimeEnum EstimateWaterFlowPattern()
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum result = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		if (mFlowRegimeCorrelation != null)
		{
			UpdateCorrelations();
			result = mFlowRegimeCorrelation.EstimateWaterFlowPattern();
		}
		else if (mStream != null && mStream.NumberOfPhases < 2)
		{
			result = ((mStream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType != PhaseType.Vapour) ? FlowRegimeEnum.Liquid : FlowRegimeEnum.Vapour);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient()
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double num = CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
		double num3 = CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
		if (num != RealValue.FEMPTY && num2 != RealValue.FEMPTY && num3 != RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			result = num + num2 + num3;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient()
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation != null)
		{
			UpdateCorrelations();
			result = mPressureGradientCorrelation.CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation, mFlowRegimeCorrelation);
			mUsedReynoldsNum = mPressureGradientCorrelation.UsedReynoldsNumber;
			mUsedFrictionFactor = mPressureGradientCorrelation.UsedFrictionFactor;
			mEquivPipeLength = mPipeLengthMultiplier * mPipeLength + PipeFlowFunctions.FittingLength(mFittingCoeffA, mFittingCoeffB, mDiameter, mFittingRoughness, mUsedFrictionFactor, mFrictionFactorMethod);
			result = result * mEquivPipeLength / mPipeLength;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient()
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation != null)
		{
			UpdateCorrelations();
			result = mPressureGradientCorrelation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation, mFlowRegimeCorrelation);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient()
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation != null)
		{
			UpdateCorrelations();
			result = mPressureGradientCorrelation.CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation, mFlowRegimeCorrelation);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp()
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation != null)
		{
			UpdateCorrelations();
			result = mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateLiquidHoldUp(mFlowRegimeCorrelation);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateWaterHoldUp()
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation != null)
		{
			UpdateCorrelations();
			result = mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateWaterHoldUp();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity()
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation != null)
		{
			UpdateCorrelations();
			result = mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateOverallDensity(mFlowRegimeCorrelation);
		}
		return result;
	}
