	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity()
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation != null)
		{
			UpdateCorrelations();
			result = mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateEquivalentDensity(mFlowRegimeCorrelation);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public bool PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		if (mPressureGradientCorrelation != null)
		{
			return mPressureGradientCorrelation.AccountsForAcceleration();
		}
		return false;
	}
}
public class PipeFlowFunctions
{
	public static double FrictionFactor(FrictionFactorMethodEnum ffMethod, double Re, double ed)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		return ffMethod switch
		{
			FrictionFactorMethodEnum.Churchill => FrictionfactorOfChurchill(Re, ed), 
			FrictionFactorMethodEnum.Moody => FrictionFactorOfMoody(Re, ed), 
			FrictionFactorMethodEnum.Round => FrictionFactorOfRound(Re, ed), 
			_ => FrictionFactorOfChen(Re, ed), 
		};
	}

	public static double TurbulentFrictionFactor(FrictionFactorMethodEnum ffMethod, double ed)
	{
		return FrictionFactor(ffMethod, 10000000000.0, ed);
	}

	public static double FrictionFactorOfMoody(double Re, double ed)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 2100.0;
		double num3 = 4000.0;
		if (Re < num2)
		{
			return 16.0 / Re;
		}
		if (Re >= num2 && Re < num3)
		{
			double num4 = 16.0 / num2;
			double num5 = FrictionFactorOfMoody(num3, ed);
			double y = Math.Log10(num4 / num5) / Math.Log10(num2 / num3);
			double num6 = num5 / Math.Pow(num3, y);
			return num6 * Math.Pow(Re, y);
		}
		double x = 20000.0 * ed + 1000000.0 / Re;
		double num7 = 0.0055 * (1.0 + Math.Pow(x, 1.0 / 3.0));
		return 0.25 * num7;
	}

	public static double FrictionfactorOfChurchill(double Re, double ed)
	{
		double y = 16.0;
		double num = 12.0;
		try
		{
			double num2 = Math.Pow(7.0 / Re, 0.9) + 0.27 * ed;
			double num3 = Math.Pow(37530.0 / Re, y);
			double num4 = Math.Pow(2.457 * Math.Log(1.0 / num2), y);
			double x = Math.Pow(8.0 / Re, num) + Math.Pow(num4 + num3, -1.5);
			double num5 = 8.0 * Math.Pow(x, 1.0 / num);
			return 0.25 * num5;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(Resources.ChurchillCorrFailed, innerException);
		}
	}

	public static double FrictionFactorOfChen(double Re, double ed)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 2100.0;
		double num3 = 4000.0;
		if (Re < num2)
		{
			return 16.0 / Re;
		}
		if (Re >= num2 && Re < num3)
		{
			double num4 = 16.0 / num2;
			double num5 = FrictionFactorOfChen(num3, ed);
			double y = Math.Log10(num4 / num5) / Math.Log10(num2 / num3);
			double num6 = num5 / Math.Pow(num3, y);
			return num6 * Math.Pow(Re, y);
		}
		double d = Math.Pow(ed, 1.1098) / 2.8257 + 5.8506 / Math.Pow(Re, 0.8981);
		double num7 = -2.0 * Math.Log10(ed / 3.7065 - 5.0452 / Re * Math.Log10(d));
		double num8 = 1.0 / (num7 * num7);
		return 0.25 * num8;
	}

	public static double FrictionFactorOfRound(double Re, double ed)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 2100.0;
		double num3 = 4000.0;
		if (Re < num2)
		{
			return 16.0 / Re;
		}
		if (Re >= num2 && Re < num3)
		{
			double num4 = 16.0 / num2;
			double num5 = FrictionFactorOfRound(num3, ed);
			double y = Math.Log10(num4 / num5) / Math.Log10(num2 / num3);
			double num6 = num5 / Math.Pow(num3, y);
			return num6 * Math.Pow(Re, y);
		}
		double d = 0.135 * ed + 6.5 / Re;
		double num7 = -1.8 * Math.Log10(d);
		double num8 = 1.0 / (num7 * num7);
		return 0.25 * num8;
	}

	public static double FrictionPressureGradientOfSinglePhaseFlow(IFlowStream stream, double diam, double roughness, FrictionFactorMethodEnum ffmethod)
	{
		IPhase phase = null;
		double num = 0.0;
		try
		{
			phase = stream.GetPhaseByID(1);
			double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * diam * diam;
			double num3 = stream.MassFlowrate.Value / num2;
			double re = num3 * diam / phase.Viscosity.Value;
			double num4 = FrictionFactor(ffmethod, re, roughness / diam);
			num = 2.0 * num4 * num3 * num3 / (phase.Density.Value * diam);
			return 0.0 - num;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "FrictionPressureGradientOfSinglePhaseFlow"), innerException);
		}
	}

	public static double FrictionPressureGradientOf2LiqPhaseFlow(IFlowStream stream, double diam, double roughness, FrictionFactorMethodEnum ffmethod)
	{
		IPhase phase = null;
		double num = 0.0;
		try
		{
			phase = stream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
			double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * diam * diam;
			double num3 = stream.MassFlowrate.Value / num2;
			double re = num3 * diam / phase.Viscosity.Value;
			double num4 = FrictionFactor(ffmethod, re, roughness / diam);
			num = 2.0 * num4 * num3 * num3 / (phase.Density.Value * diam);
			return 0.0 - num;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "FrictionPressureGradientOf2LiqPhaseFlow"), innerException);
		}
	}

	public static double GetWallisBubbleVelocity(IFlowStream stream, double diameter)
	{
		double fEMPTY = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = stream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		IPhase phaseByType = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		if (combinedLiquidPhase == null || phaseByType == null || diameter <= 0.0)
		{
			return fEMPTY;
		}
		double value = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
		double value2 = phaseByType.Density.Value;
		double value3 = combinedLiquidPhase.SurfaceTension.Value;
		double value4 = combinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value;
		double num = 9.80665 * diameter * diameter * (value - value2) / value3;
		double num2 = Math.Sqrt(9.80665 * value * (value - value2) * diameter * diameter * diameter) / value4;
		double num3 = 1.0;
		if (num2 <= 18.0)
		{
			num3 = 2.5;
		}
		else if (num2 > 18.0 && num2 < 250.0)
		{
			num3 = 69.0 / Math.Pow(num2, 0.35);
		}
		else if (num2 >= 250.0)
		{
			num3 = 10.0;
		}
		double num4 = 0.345 * (1.0 - Math.Exp(-0.029 * num2)) * (1.0 - Math.Exp((3.37 - num) / num3));
		return num4 * 9.80665 * diameter * (value - value2) / value;
	}

	public static double FittingLength(double a, double b, double diam, double rough, double ff, FrictionFactorMethodEnum ffmethod)
	{
		double num = TurbulentFrictionFactor(ffmethod, rough / diam);
		double result = 0.0;
		if (ff != 0.0)
		{
			result = (0.25 * a + b * num) * diam / ff;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public static double GetFlanaganEquivalentDensity(IFlowStream stream, double diameter)
	{
		double fEMPTY = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double fEMPTY2 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		IPhase phaseByType = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = stream.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		if (phaseByType == null || combinedLiquidPhase == null)
		{
			return fEMPTY2;
		}
		double num = stream.MassFlowrate.Value * phaseByType.MassFraction.Value / phaseByType.Density.Value;
		double x = num / (Math.PI * diameter * diameter * 0.25);
		fEMPTY = combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
		double num2 = 1.0 / (1.0 + Math.Pow(x, 1.006));
		return num2 * fEMPTY;
	}

	public static double GetMaximumEnthalpyChange(IFlowStream testStream, double extTemp, IPhysicalPropertyPackage pIPPP)
	{
		double result = 1E+20;
		if (extTemp != RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			IFlowStream flowStream = (IFlowStream)testStream.Clone();
			double value = testStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
			FlashCalculationLevelEnum flashLevel = pIPPP.FlashLevel;
			pIPPP.FlashLevel = FlashCalculationLevelEnum.Thermal;
			pIPPP.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(flowStream, FlashType.PTFlash, value, extTemp);
			pIPPP.FlashLevel = flashLevel;
			double value2 = testStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
			double value3 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
			result = Math.Abs(value3 - value2);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public static void getChokingCondition(IPhysicalPropertyPackage pIPPP, IFlowStream refstream, IFlowStream tgtstream, double Dtgt, double Dmin, int methodType, double requiredAccuracy, double StepwiseFactor)
	{
		IFlowStream flowStream = (IFlowStream)refstream.Clone();
		double fEMPTY = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double value = tgtstream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
		try
		{
			fEMPTY = HTFSUtility.GetChockedPressure(flowStream, pIPPP, Dmin);
			pIPPP.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(flowStream, FlashType.PHFlash, fEMPTY, value);
			if (Dmin <= Dtgt)
			{
				double num = fEMPTY;
				double area = HTFSUtility.FlowArea(Dmin);
				double num2 = HTFSUtility.CalculateMassFlux(flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value, area);
				double area2 = HTFSUtility.FlowArea(Dtgt);
				double num3 = HTFSUtility.CalculateMassFlux(flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value, area2);
				double num4;
				double num5;
				if (methodType == 1)
				{
					num4 = Crane.CalculateHomogeneousEquivalentDensity(flowStream);
					num5 = num4;
				}
				else
				{
					num4 = HTFSUtility.CalculateEquivalentDensity(flowStream, num2, 0.0);
					num5 = HTFSUtility.CalculateEquivalentDensity(flowStream, num3, 0.0);
				}
				double num6 = num4;
				double num7 = num4;
				while (num6 > requiredAccuracy * num4)
				{
					double num8 = 0.5 * (num2 * num2 / num4 - num3 * num3 / num5);
					fEMPTY = num + num8 * StepwiseFactor;
					pIPPP.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(flowStream, FlashType.PHFlash, fEMPTY, value);
					num5 = ((methodType != 1) ? HTFSUtility.CalculateEquivalentDensity(flowStream, num3, 0.0) : Crane.CalculateHomogeneousEquivalentDensity(flowStream));
					num6 = Math.Abs(num5 - num7);
					num7 = num5;
				}
			}
			tgtstream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "estimateUpstreamCondition"), innerException);
		}
	}

	public static void getChokingUpstreamCondition(IPhysicalPropertyPackage pIPPP, IFlowStream refstream, IFlowStream tgtstream, double Dtgt, double Dmin, int methodType, double requiredAccuracy)
	{
		IFlowStream flowStream = (IFlowStream)refstream.Clone();
		double fEMPTY = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double value = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
		try
		{
			fEMPTY = HTFSUtility.GetChockedPressure(flowStream, pIPPP, Dmin);
			pIPPP.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(flowStream, FlashType.PHFlash, fEMPTY, value);
			if (Dmin <= Dtgt)
			{
				double num = fEMPTY;
				double value2 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value;
				double area = HTFSUtility.FlowArea(Dmin);
				double num2 = HTFSUtility.CalculateMassFlux(flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value, area);
				double area2 = HTFSUtility.FlowArea(Dtgt);
				double num3 = HTFSUtility.CalculateMassFlux(flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value, area2);
				double num4;
				double num5;
				if (methodType == 1)
				{
					num4 = Crane.CalculateHomogeneousEquivalentDensity(flowStream);
					num5 = num4;
				}
				else
				{
					num4 = HTFSUtility.CalculateEquivalentDensity(flowStream, num2, 0.0);
					num5 = HTFSUtility.CalculateEquivalentDensity(flowStream, num3, 0.0);
				}
				double num6 = num4;
				double num7 = num4;
				while (num6 > requiredAccuracy * num4)
				{
					double num8 = 0.5 * (num2 * num2 / num4 - num3 * num3 / num5);
					fEMPTY = num + num8;
					pIPPP.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(flowStream, FlashType.PSFlash, fEMPTY, value2);
					num5 = ((methodType != 1) ? HTFSUtility.CalculateEquivalentDensity(flowStream, num3, 0.0) : Crane.CalculateHomogeneousEquivalentDensity(flowStream));
					num6 = Math.Abs(num5 - num7);
					num7 = num5;
				}
			}
			tgtstream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "estimateUpstreamCondition"), innerException);
		}
	}

	public static bool isExcessSpeedOfSound(IPhysicalPropertyPackage pIPPP, IFlowStream stream, double area)
	{
		try
		{
			if (((IBlackOilStream)stream).IsBlackOil)
			{
				return false;
			}
			double num = HTFSUtility.CalculateMassFlux(stream.MassFlowrate.Value, area);
			double num2 = Crane.CalculateHomogeneousEquivalentDensity(stream);
			double num3 = num / num2;
			double num4 = stream.GetPhaseByID(0).SonicVelocity.Value;
			if (num4 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
			{
				num4 = HTFSUtility.CalcSonicVelocity(pIPPP, stream);
				stream.GetPhaseByID(0).SonicVelocity.Value = num4;
			}
			if (num4 != RealValue.FEMPTY && num3 > num4)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "isExcessSpeedOfSound"), innerException);
		}
	}
}
public class PipeFlowHandler : FluidFlowHandler, IPipeFlow, IFluidFlow
{
	private RealProperty mDiam;

	private RealProperty mAngle;

	private RealProperty mRoughness;

	private RealProperty mVF;

	private RealProperty mPG;

	private BooleanProperty mChoked;

	private FlowRegimeEnum mPattern;

	private FlowRegimeMethodEnum mFRMethod;

	private VoidFractionMethodEnum mVFMethod;

	private PressureGradientMethodEnum mPGMethod;

	private bool mVFUpdated;

	private bool mFRUpdated;

	private bool mPGUpdated;

	public override IPhysicalPropertyPackage PPPOfFluid
	{
		get
		{
			try
			{
				return mIPPP;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
		}
		set
		{
			mIPPP = value;
			initialiseBoolenVariable();
		}
	}

	public override IFlowStream FlowStream
	{
		get
		{
			try
			{
				return mStream;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
		}
		set
		{
			mStream = value;
			initialiseBoolenVariable();
		}
	}

	public RealProperty Diameter
	{
		get
		{
			try
			{
				return mDiam;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "getPropertyDiameter"), innerException);
			}
		}
		set
		{
			try
			{
				mDiam = value;
				initialiseBoolenVariable();
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "setPropertyDiameter"), innerException);
			}
		}
	}

	public RealProperty InclinedAngle
	{
		get
		{
			return mAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mAngle = value;
			initialiseBoolenVariable();
		}
	}

	public RealProperty Roughness
	{
		get
		{
			return mRoughness;
		}
		set
		{
			mRoughness = value;
			initialiseBoolenVariable();
		}
	}

	public RealProperty VoidFraction
	{
		get
		{
			if (!mVFUpdated)
			{
				calculateVoidFraction();
			}
			return mVF;
		}
	}

	public RealProperty PGradient
	{
		get
		{
			if (!mPGUpdated)
			{
				calculatePressureGradient();
			}
			return mPG;
		}
	}

	public BooleanProperty IsChoked
	{
		get
		{
			return mChoked;
		}
		set
		{
			mChoked = value;
		}
	}

	public FlowRegimeEnum FlowRegime
	{
		get
		{
			if (!mFRUpdated)
			{
				getFlowPattern();
			}
			return mPattern;
		}
	}

	public FlowRegimeMethodEnum FlowRegimeMethod
	{
		get
		{
			return mFRMethod;
		}
		set
		{
			mFRMethod = value;
			mFRUpdated = false;
		}
	}

	public VoidFractionMethodEnum VoidFractionMethod
	{
		get
		{
			return mVFMethod;
		}
		set
		{
			mVFMethod = value;
			mVFUpdated = false;
		}
	}

	public PressureGradientMethodEnum PressureGradientMethod
	{
		get
		{
			return mPGMethod;
		}
		set
		{
			mPGMethod = value;
			mPGUpdated = false;
		}
	}

	public PipeFlowHandler()
	{
		initPipeFlowHandler();
	}

	public PipeFlowHandler(IFlowStream Stream, double diam, double inclinedAngle, double roughness)
		: this()
	{
		initParameters(Stream, diam, inclinedAngle, roughness);
	}

	public double CalculateVoidFraction(IFlowStream stream, double diam, double inclinedAngle, double roughness, VoidFractionMethodEnum method)
	{
		try
		{
			initParameters(stream, diam, inclinedAngle, roughness);
			mVFMethod = method;
			calculateVoidFraction();
			return mVF.Value;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "CalculateVoidFraction"), innerException);
		}
	}

	public double CalculateDP_DLInPipe(IFlowStream stream, double diam, double inclinedAngle, double roughness, VoidFractionMethodEnum vfcor, PressureGradientMethodEnum pgcor, ref bool isChoked)
	{
		try
		{
			initParameters(stream, diam, inclinedAngle, roughness);
			mVFMethod = vfcor;
			mPGMethod = pgcor;
			calculatePressureGradient();
			isChoked = mChoked.Value;
			return mPG.Value;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "CalculateDP_DLInPipe"), innerException);
		}
	}

	public FlowRegimeEnum EstimateFlowPattern(IFlowStream stream, double diam, double inclinedAngle, FlowRegimeMethodEnum method)
	{
		try
		{
			initParameters(stream, diam, inclinedAngle, mRoughness.Value);
			mFRMethod = method;
			getFlowPattern();
			return mPattern;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "EstimateFlowPattern"), innerException);
		}
	}

	private void initPipeFlowHandler()
	{
		mDiam = new RealProperty();
		mDiam.Description = Resources.PipeDiamMeter;
		mDiam.DefaultValue = 0.0;
		mDiam.LBound = 0.0;
		mDiam.UBound = 10.0;
		mDiam.Value = 0.0;
		mDiam.AfterValueChanged += ReinitialiseBoolenVariable;
		mAngle = new RealProperty();
		mAngle.Description = Resources.PipeAngleDeg;
		mAngle.DefaultValue = 0.0;
		mAngle.LBound = -90.0;
		mAngle.UBound = 90.0;
		mAngle.Value = 0.0;
		mAngle.AfterValueChanged += ReinitialiseBoolenVariable;
		mRoughness = new RealProperty();
		mRoughness.Description = Resources.PipeRoughMeter;
		mRoughness.DefaultValue = 0.0;
		mRoughness.LBound = 0.0;
		mRoughness.UBound = 0.05;
		mRoughness.Value = 0.0;
		mRoughness.AfterValueChanged += ReinitialiseBoolenVariable;
		mVF = new RealProperty();
		mVF.Description = Resources.VoidFracPct;
		mVF.DefaultValue = 0.0;
		mVF.LBound = 0.0;
		mVF.UBound = 1.0;
		mVF.Value = 0.0;
		mPG = new RealProperty();
		mPG.Description = Resources.PGradPaPerM;
		mPG.DefaultValue = 0.0;
		mPG.Value = 0.0;
		mChoked = new BooleanProperty();
		mChoked.Description = Resources.FlowChokedOrNot;
		mChoked.DefaultValue = false;
		mChoked.Value = false;
		mPattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Liquid;
		mFRMethod = FlowRegimeMethodEnum.Default;
		mVFMethod = VoidFractionMethodEnum.BeggsAndBrill;
		mPGMethod = PressureGradientMethodEnum.BeggsAndBrill;
		initialiseBoolenVariable();
	}

	private void initialiseBoolenVariable()
	{
		mVFUpdated = false;
		mFRUpdated = false;
		mPGUpdated = false;
	}

	private void initParameters(IFlowStream Stream, double diam, double inclinedAngle, double roughness)
	{
		mStream = Stream;
		mDiam.Value = diam;
		mAngle.Value = inclinedAngle;
		mRoughness.Value = roughness;
	}

	private void getFlowPattern()
	{
		try
		{
			FlowRegimeMethodEnum flowRegimeMethodEnum = mFRMethod;
			_ = flowRegimeMethodEnum - -1;
			_ = 1;
			mPattern = PipeFlow.FlowRegimeOfBeggsAndBrill(mStream, mDiam.Value, out var _, out var _, out var _);
			mFRUpdated = true;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "getFlowPattern"), innerException);
		}
	}

	private void calculateVoidFraction()
	{
		try
		{
			if (mStream.NumberOfPhases < 2)
			{
				if (mStream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid)
				{
					mVF.Value = 0.0;
				}
				else
				{
					mVF.Value = 1.0;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				VoidFractionMethodEnum voidFractionMethodEnum = mVFMethod;
				if (voidFractionMethodEnum == VoidFractionMethodEnum.Default || voidFractionMethodEnum == VoidFractionMethodEnum.BeggsAndBrill)
				{
					mVF.Value = PipeFlow.VoidFractionOfBeggsAndBrill(mStream, mDiam.Value, mAngle.Value);
				}
			}
			mVFUpdated = true;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "calculateVoidFraction"), innerException);
		}
	}

	private void calculatePressureGradient()
	{
		try
		{
			if (mStream.NumberOfPhases < 2)
			{
				mPG.Value = PipeFlow.PressreGradientOfSinglePhaseFlow(mStream, mDiam.Value, mAngle.Value, mRoughness.Value);
				if (mStream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					IPhase phaseByID = mStream.GetPhaseByID(1);
					double num = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value / (phaseByID.Density.Value * 0.5 * Math.PI * mDiam.Value * mDiam.Value);
					double num2 = num * num * phaseByID.Density.Value / phaseByID.Pressure.Value;
					mPG.Value /= 1.0 - num2;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				PressureGradientMethodEnum pressureGradientMethodEnum = mPGMethod;
				if (pressureGradientMethodEnum != PressureGradientMethodEnum.Default)
				{
					_ = 3;
				}
				mPG.Value = PipeFlow.PressreGradientOfBeggsAndBrill(mStream, mDiam.Value, mAngle.Value, mRoughness.Value);
			}
			mPGUpdated = true;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "calculatePressureGradient"), innerException);
		}
	}

	private void ReinitialiseBoolenVariable(object Sender, double OldValue)
	{
		initialiseBoolenVariable();
	}
}
public class PressureDropCurveCalculation : ICorrelationManager
{
	public enum DensityMethod
	{
		Incompressible,
		IdealGas
	}

	private IPressureDropCurveCollection mDPCurves;

	private IFlowStream mStream;

	private DensityMethod mDensityMethod;

	private double mPref;

	private bool mPressureCorrection;

	public DensityMethod DensityCorrectionMethod
	{
		get
		{
			return mDensityMethod;
		}
		set
		{
			mDensityMethod = value;
		}
	}

	public double ReferencePressure
	{
		get
		{
			return mPref;
		}
		set
		{
			mPref = value;
		}
	}

	public bool PressureCorrection
	{
		get
		{
			return mPressureCorrection;
		}
		set
		{
			mPressureCorrection = value;
		}
	}

	public IPressureDropCurveCollection PressureDropCurves
	{
		set
		{
			mDPCurves = value;
		}
	}

	public IFlowStream FlowStream
	{
		get
		{
			return mStream;
		}
		set
		{
			mStream = value;
		}
	}

	public PressureDropCurveCalculation()
	{
		mStream = null;
		mDPCurves = null;
		mPref = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mPressureCorrection = true;
		mDensityMethod = DensityMethod.IdealGas;
	}

	public PressureDropCurveCalculation(IFlowStream stream, IPressureDropCurveCollection curves)
	{
		mStream = stream;
		mDPCurves = curves;
		mPref = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mPressureCorrection = true;
		mDensityMethod = DensityMethod.IdealGas;
	}

	public void AddCurve(IPressureDropCurve curve)
	{
		int num = mDPCurves.Add(curve);
	}

	public void RemoveCurve(IPressureDropCurve curve)
	{
		mDPCurves.Remove(curve);
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient()
	{
		double num = CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
		double num3 = CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
		return num + num2 + num3;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient()
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		if (mStream == null || mDPCurves == null)
		{
			return num;
		}
		double value = mStream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolecularWeight.Value;
		double massFlow = Math.Abs(mStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
		double pressureDrop = mDPCurves.GetPressureDrop(value, massFlow);
		if (mDensityMethod == DensityMethod.Incompressible)
		{
			num = pressureDrop;
		}
		else if (mDensityMethod == DensityMethod.IdealGas)
		{
			double num2 = mPref;
			double num3 = mDPCurves.ReferenceTemperature;
			double value2 = mStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
			if (num3 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
			{
				num3 = value2;
			}
			if (PressureCorrection)
			{
				double num4 = 1.0;
				double num5 = num2;
				double num6 = (0.0 - (num2 + pressureDrop)) * pressureDrop * value2 / num3;
				num = (0.0 - num5 + Math.Sqrt(num5 * num5 - 4.0 * num4 * num6)) / (2.0 * num4);
			}
			else
			{
				num = pressureDrop * (value2 / num3);
			}
		}
		return 0.0 - num;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (mDensityMethod == DensityMethod.Incompressible)
		{
			result = 0.0;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity()
	{
		HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation homogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		return homogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity()
	{
		HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation homogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		return homogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public bool PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		bool result = false;
		if (mDensityMethod == DensityMethod.Incompressible)
		{
			result = true;
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public delegate double CalculateMomentumEnergyDelegate(IFlowStream stream, double diam);
public enum GradientEstimateMethod

	UseKnownStream,
	UseUnknownStream,
	UseAverage
}

	private double mOutMassFlow;

	private MessageLogger mLog;

	public double FrictionPressureDrop => mFrictionPressureDrop;

	public PipeFlowPressureSolver()
	{
		mAccuracy = 0.001;
		mMaxPresIter = 100;
		mCorrelation = null;
		mHeatFlux = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mHTC = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mRto = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mExternalTemp = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mInDiameter = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mOutDiameter = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mPhysPropPkg = null;
		mCalculateMomentumEnergy = null;
		mGradientMethod = GradientEstimateMethod.UseAverage;
		mIncludeAcceleration = AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Include;
		mIgnoreDecceleration = false;
		mIncludeMomentumEnergy = true;
		mCalcEnthalpyChange = true;
		mStaticHeadMethod = StaticHeadContributionEnum.Include;
		mInMassFlow = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mOutMassFlow = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mFrictionPressureDrop = 0.0;
		mGravityPressureDrop = 0.0;
		mAccelerationPressureDrop = 0.0;
		mRatedFlowRatio = 1.0;
		mLog = null;
	}

	public abstract bool IsReady();

	public abstract bool CalculateUnknownFlowStream(IFlowStream unknownStream, IFlowStream knownStream, double length, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor);

	public bool CalculateUnknownStream(IFlowStream unknownStream, IFlowStream knownStream, double length, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{
		knownStream.MassFlowrate.Value *= RatedFlowRatio;
		unknownStream.MassFlowrate.Value *= RatedFlowRatio;
		bool result = CalculateUnknownFlowStream(unknownStream, knownStream, length, StepwiseFactor, FlowDirectionFactor);
		knownStream.MassFlowrate.Value /= RatedFlowRatio;
		unknownStream.MassFlowrate.Value /= RatedFlowRatio;
		return result;
	}

	public void SetHeatTransferParameters(double htc, double Rto, double extTemp)
	{
		mHTC = htc;
		mRto = Rto;
		mExternalTemp = extTemp;
	}

	public void SetGeometricalParameters(double inDiam, double outDiam, double angle)
	{
		mInDiameter = inDiam;
		mOutDiameter = outDiam;
		mAngle = angle;
	}

	public void SetMassFlowrates(double inMassFlow, double outMassFlow)
	{
		mInMassFlow = inMassFlow;
		mOutMassFlow = outMassFlow;
	}

	public double InletMassFlux()
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (mInMassFlow != RealValue.FEMPTY && mInDiameter > 0.0)
		{
			result = mInMassFlow / (Math.PI / 4.0 * mInDiameter * mInDiameter);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double OutletMassFlux()
	{
		double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (mOutMassFlow != RealValue.FEMPTY && mOutDiameter > 0.0)
		{
			result = mOutMassFlow / (Math.PI / 4.0 * mOutDiameter * mOutDiameter);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double GetAccelerationPressureChange(double inMassFlux, double inDensity, double outMassFlux, double outDensity)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (inDensity != 0.0 && outDensity != 0.0)
		{
			result = ((inMassFlux == outMassFlux) ? (outMassFlux * outMassFlux / outDensity - inMassFlux * inMassFlux / inDensity) : (0.5 * (outMassFlux * outMassFlux / outDensity - inMassFlux * inMassFlux / inDensity)));
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double GetEnthalpyChange(IFlowStream inStream, IFlowStream outStream, double inDensity, double outDensity, double inHeatFlux, double outHeatFlux, double inDiameter, double outDiameter, double length, double angle, double maxDH)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		if (Math.Abs(inHeatFlux) > 0.0 || Math.Abs(outHeatFlux) > 0.0)
		{
			double num2 = Math.PI / 2.0 * length * (inDiameter + outDiameter);
			num = 0.5 * (inHeatFlux / InletMassFlow + outHeatFlux / OutletMassFlow) * num2;
			if (Math.Abs(num) > Math.Abs(maxDH))
			{
				num = Math.Abs(maxDH) * (double)Math.Sign(num);
			}
		}
		double num3 = 0.0;
		if (IncludeMomentumEnergy)
		{
			double num4 = 0.0;
			double num5 = 0.0;
			if (mCalculateMomentumEnergy == null)
			{
				double num6 = InletMassFlux();
				double num7 = OutletMassFlux();
				num4 = 0.5 * num6 * num6 / (inDensity * inDensity);
				num5 = 0.5 * num7 * num7 / (outDensity * outDensity);
			}
			else
			{
				num4 = mCalculateMomentumEnergy(inStream, inDiameter);
				num5 = mCalculateMomentumEnergy(outStream, outDiameter);
			}
			num3 = num5 - num4;
		}
		return num + num3 + 9.80665 * length * Math.Sin(HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(angle));
	}

	protected virtual int maxLoopCounter()
	{
		return MaxPresIter;
	}

	protected double CalculateAccelerationGradient(IFlowStream unknownStream, IFlowStream knownStream, IFlowStream refStream, double unknownDensity, double knownDensity, double inMassFlux, double outMassFlux, double length, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		bool flag = refStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 || refStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null;
		switch (IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop)
		{
		case AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Ignore:
			num = 0.0;
			break;
		case AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Include:
			if (!Correlation.PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration() || flag)
			{
				num = ((StepwiseFactor != 1.0) ? ((0.0 - GetAccelerationPressureChange(inMassFlux, knownDensity, outMassFlux, unknownDensity)) / length) : ((0.0 - GetAccelerationPressureChange(inMassFlux, unknownDensity, outMassFlux, knownDensity)) / length));
				break;
			}
			Correlation.FlowStream = refStream;
			num = Correlation.CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
			break;
		case AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Homogeneous:
		{
			double num2 = Crane.CalculateHomogeneousEquivalentDensity(unknownStream);
			double num3 = Crane.CalculateHomogeneousEquivalentDensity(knownStream);
			num = ((StepwiseFactor != 1.0) ? ((0.0 - GetAccelerationPressureChange(inMassFlux, num3, outMassFlux, num2)) / length) : ((0.0 - GetAccelerationPressureChange(inMassFlux, num2, outMassFlux, num3)) / length));
			break;
		}
		}
		if (IgnoreDeccelerationPressureDrop && FlowDirectionFactor * num > 0.0 && !flag)
		{
			num = 0.0;
		}
		return num;
	}

	protected double CalculateAdditionalAccelerationPressureDrop(IFlowStream knownStream, IFlowStream unknownStream, double knownDensity, double unknownDensity, double inMassFlux, double outMassFlux, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		switch (IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop)
		{
		case AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Ignore:
			num = 0.0;
			break;
		case AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Include:
		{
			bool flag = (knownStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 || knownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null) && (unknownStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 || unknownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null);
			num = ((Correlation.PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration() && !flag) ? 0.0 : ((StepwiseFactor != 1.0) ? GetAccelerationPressureChange(inMassFlux, knownDensity, outMassFlux, unknownDensity) : GetAccelerationPressureChange(inMassFlux, unknownDensity, outMassFlux, knownDensity)));
			break;
		}
		case AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Homogeneous:
		{
			double num2 = Crane.CalculateHomogeneousEquivalentDensity(unknownStream);
			double num3 = Crane.CalculateHomogeneousEquivalentDensity(knownStream);
			num = ((StepwiseFactor != 1.0) ? GetAccelerationPressureChange(inMassFlux, num3, outMassFlux, num2) : GetAccelerationPressureChange(inMassFlux, num2, outMassFlux, num3));
			break;
		}
		}
		if (IgnoreDeccelerationPressureDrop && FlowDirectionFactor * num < 0.0)
		{
			num = 0.0;
		}
		return num;
	}

	protected double CalculateDeltaEnthalpy(IFlowStream knownStream, IFlowStream unknownStream, double StepwiseFactor, double dz, double maxDH, double knownDensity, double unknownDensity, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{
		double fEMPTY = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		double fEMPTY2 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		if (HeatFlux != RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			fEMPTY = (fEMPTY2 = HeatFlux);
		}
		else
		{
			double thermalResistance = ExternalThermalResistance + Conduction.ThermalResistanceForConvection(HeatTransferCoeff, Math.PI * InletDiameter);
			double thermalResistance2 = ExternalThermalResistance + Conduction.ThermalResistanceForConvection(HeatTransferCoeff, Math.PI * OutletDiameter);
			double num = Math.PI * InletDiameter;
			double num2 = Math.PI * OutletDiameter;
			if (StepwiseFactor == 1.0)
			{
				fEMPTY = Conduction.HeatFlux(knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value, ExternalTemperature, thermalResistance2) / num2;
				fEMPTY2 = Conduction.HeatFlux(unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value, ExternalTemperature, thermalResistance) / num;
			}
			else
			{
				fEMPTY = Conduction.HeatFlux(knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value, ExternalTemperature, thermalResistance) / num;
				fEMPTY2 = Conduction.HeatFlux(unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value, ExternalTemperature, thermalResistance2) / num2;
			}
		}
		double num3 = 0.0;
		if (StepwiseFactor == 1.0)
		{
			return GetEnthalpyChange(unknownStream, knownStream, unknownDensity, knownDensity, 0.0 - fEMPTY2, 0.0 - fEMPTY, InletDiameter, OutletDiameter, dz, InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor, maxDH);
		}
		return GetEnthalpyChange(knownStream, unknownStream, knownDensity, unknownDensity, 0.0 - fEMPTY, 0.0 - fEMPTY2, InletDiameter, OutletDiameter, dz, InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor, maxDH);
	}
}
public class PipeFlowPressureSolverLengthMethod : PipeFlowPressureSolver
{
	private int mNumSegments;

	public int NumberOfSegments
	{
		get
		{
			return mNumSegments;
		}
		set
		{
			mNumSegments = value;
		}
	}

	public PipeFlowPressureSolverLengthMethod()
	{
		mNumSegments = 1;
	}

	public override bool IsReady()
	{
		if (mNumSegments < 1)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.InletDiameter <= 0.0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.OutletDiameter <= 0.0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.InclinationAngle == RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.HeatFlux == RealValue.FEMPTY && (base.HeatTransferCoeff == RealValue.FEMPTY || base.ExternalThermalResistance == RealValue.FEMPTY || base.ExternalTemperature == RealValue.FEMPTY))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.Correlation == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.PhysicalPropertyPackage == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public override bool CalculateUnknownFlowStream(IFlowStream unknownStream, IFlowStream knownStream, double length, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 0.0;
		double num3 = 0.0;
		double num4 = 0.0;
		double num5 = 0.0;
		double num6 = 0.0;
		double num7 = 0.0;
		double num8 = 0.0;
		double num9 = 0.0;
		double num10 = 0.0;
		double num11 = 0.0;
		double num12 = 0.0;
		double num13 = -32767.0;
		if (!IsReady())
		{
			return false;
		}
		try
		{
			IFlowStream flowStream = (IFlowStream)knownStream.Clone();
			if (StepwiseFactor == 1.0)
			{
				SetMassFlowrates(unknownStream.MassFlowrate.Value, flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
			}
			else
			{
				SetMassFlowrates(flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value, unknownStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
			}
			double inMassFlux = InletMassFlux();
			double outMassFlux = OutletMassFlux();
			int num14 = 0;
			double num15 = 0.0;
			double num16 = length / (double)NumberOfSegments;
			mFrictionPressureDrop = 0.0;
			mGravityPressureDrop = 0.0;
			mAccelerationPressureDrop = 0.0;
			base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(unknownStream, FlashType.PHFlash, flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value, unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value);
			while (num15 < length)
			{
				num14++;
				num13 = 0.0;
				if (num15 == 0.0)
				{
					base.Correlation.FlowStream = flowStream;
					num11 = base.Correlation.CalculateEquivalentDensity();
					num3 = base.Correlation.CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
					switch (base.StaticHeadMethod)
					{
					case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Include:
						num5 = base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
						break;
					case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Ignore:
						num5 = 0.0;
						break;
					case StaticHeadContributionEnum.IgnoreDownhill:
						num5 = ((!(base.InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor >= 0.0)) ? 0.0 : base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient());
						break;
					}
					if (base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop == AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Include)
					{
						if (flowStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 || flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
						{
							base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop = AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Homogeneous;
							num7 = 0.0;
						}
						else
						{
							num7 = base.Correlation.CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						num7 = 0.0;
					}
					num = num3 + num5 + num7;
				}
				else
				{
					flowStream = (IFlowStream)unknownStream.Clone();
					num11 = num12;
					num = num2;
					num3 = num4;
					num5 = num6;
					num7 = num8;
				}
				double num17 = 1E+20;
				double num18 = 0.0;
				double num19 = 0.0;
				double maxDH = 1E+20;
				if (base.HeatFlux != 0.0)
				{
					maxDH = PipeFlowFunctions.GetMaximumEnthalpyChange(flowStream, base.ExternalTemperature, base.PhysicalPropertyPackage);
				}
				int num20 = 0;
				while (num17 > Math.Abs(base.Accuracy * num18) && (num18 == 0.0 || !(Math.Abs(num18) < base.Accuracy)) && num20 < maxLoopCounter())
				{
					num20++;
					base.Correlation.FlowStream = unknownStream;
					switch (base.GradientEstimate)
					{
					case GradientEstimateMethod.UseKnownStream:
						num2 = num;
						num4 = num3;
						num6 = num5;
						num8 = num7;
						break;
					case GradientEstimateMethod.UseAverage:
						num4 = base.Correlation.CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
						switch (base.StaticHeadMethod)
						{
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Include:
							num6 = base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
							break;
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Ignore:
							num6 = 0.0;
							break;
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.IgnoreDownhill:
							num6 = ((!(base.InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor >= 0.0)) ? 0.0 : base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient());
							break;
						}
						if (base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop == AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Include)
						{
							if (unknownStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 || unknownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
							{
								base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop = AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Homogeneous;
								num8 = 0.0;
								num -= num7;
								num7 = 0.0;
							}
							else
							{
								num8 = base.Correlation.CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
							}
						}
						else
						{
							num8 = 0.0;
						}
						num2 = num4 + num6 + num8;
						break;
					case GradientEstimateMethod.UseUnknownStream:
						num4 = base.Correlation.CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
						switch (base.StaticHeadMethod)
						{
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Include:
							num6 = base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
							break;
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Ignore:
							num6 = 0.0;
							break;
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.IgnoreDownhill:
							num6 = ((!(base.InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor >= 0.0)) ? 0.0 : base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient());
							break;
						}
						if (base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop == AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Include)
						{
							if (unknownStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 || unknownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
							{
								base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop = AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Homogeneous;
								num8 = 0.0;
							}
							else
							{
								num8 = base.Correlation.CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
							}
						}
						else
						{
							num8 = 0.0;
						}
						num2 = num4 + num6 + num8;
						num = num2;
						num3 = num4;
						num5 = num6;
						num7 = num8;
						break;
					}
					num12 = base.Correlation.CalculateEquivalentDensity();
					num13 = CalculateAdditionalAccelerationPressureDrop(flowStream, unknownStream, num11, num12, inMassFlux, outMassFlux, StepwiseFactor, FlowDirectionFactor);
					num9 = 0.5 * FlowDirectionFactor * (num + num2) * num16 - num13;
					num17 = Math.Abs(num9 - num18);
					num10 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value + num9 * (0.0 - StepwiseFactor);
					if (num10 < 0.0)
					{
						if (num13 * (0.0 - StepwiseFactor) > flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value)
						{
							num13 = 0.0;
							num9 = 0.5 * FlowDirectionFactor * (num + num2) * num16 - num13;
							base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop = AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Ignore;
							base.IncludeMomentumEnergy = false;
						}
						else
						{
							num9 = 0.0;
							if (base.Log != null)
							{
								base.Log.Add(MessageLogType.Error, Resources.NegativePressureContinueWithZeroDP);
							}
						}
						num10 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value + num9 * (0.0 - StepwiseFactor);
					}
					num19 = 0.0;
					if (base.CalculateEnthalpyChange)
					{
						num19 = CalculateDeltaEnthalpy(flowStream, unknownStream, StepwiseFactor, num16, maxDH, num11, num12, FlowDirectionFactor);
					}
					double data = ((num19 != 0.0) ? (flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value + StepwiseFactor * num19) : unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value);
					base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(unknownStream, FlashType.PHFlash, num10, data);
					if (num20 > maxLoopCounter())
					{
						if (base.Log != null)
						{
							base.Log.Add(MessageLogType.Error, Resources.FailedToSolveInIterationLimit);
						}
						throw new Exception("PipeCell.CalculateDPWithHTFSMethod " + Resources.FailedToSolveInIterationLimit);
					}
					num18 = num9;
				}
				if (num20 >= maxLoopCounter() && base.Log != null && num18 == 0.0)
				{
					base.Log.Add(MessageLogType.Error, Resources.FailedToSolveInIterationLimit);
					base.Log.AddDiagnostic("FailIteration0dP", 200);
				}
				num15 += num16;
				mFrictionPressureDrop += -0.5 * (num3 + num4) * FlowDirectionFactor * num16;
				mGravityPressureDrop += -0.5 * (num5 + num6) * FlowDirectionFactor * num16;
				mAccelerationPressureDrop += -0.5 * (num7 + num8) * FlowDirectionFactor * num16 + num13;
			}
			return true;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "CalculateOutletStream " + Resources.WithLength), innerException);
		}
	}
}
public class PipeFlowPressureSolverAveragePropertiesMethod : PipeFlowPressureSolver
{
	private int mNumSegments;

	private bool mDoInnerLoop;

	public int NumberOfSegments
	{
		get
		{

		}
		set

		}
	}

	public bool DoInnerLoop
	{
		get
		{
			return mDoInnerLoop;
		}
		set
		{
			mDoInnerLoop = value;
		}
	}

	public PipeFlowPressureSolverAveragePropertiesMethod()
	{
		mNumSegments = 1;
		mDoInnerLoop = true;
	}

	public override bool IsReady()
	{
		if (mNumSegments < 1)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.InletDiameter <= 0.0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.OutletDiameter <= 0.0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.InclinationAngle == RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.HeatFlux == RealValue.FEMPTY && (base.HeatTransferCoeff == RealValue.FEMPTY || base.ExternalThermalResistance == RealValue.FEMPTY || base.ExternalTemperature == RealValue.FEMPTY))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.Correlation == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.PhysicalPropertyPackage == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public override bool CalculateUnknownFlowStream(IFlowStream unknownStream, IFlowStream knownStream, double length, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 0.0;
		double num3 = 0.0;
		double num4 = 0.0;
		double num5 = 0.0;
		double num6 = 0.0;
		double num7 = 0.0;
		double num8 = 0.0;
		double num9 = 0.0;
		if (!IsReady())
		{
			return false;
		}
		try
		{
			IFlowStream flowStream = (IFlowStream)knownStream.Clone();
			if (StepwiseFactor == 1.0)
			{
				SetMassFlowrates(unknownStream.MassFlowrate.Value, flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
			}
			else
			{
				SetMassFlowrates(flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value, unknownStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
			}
			double inMassFlux = InletMassFlux();
			double outMassFlux = OutletMassFlux();
			double num10 = 0.0;
			double num11 = length / (double)NumberOfSegments;
			mFrictionPressureDrop = 0.0;
			mGravityPressureDrop = 0.0;
			mAccelerationPressureDrop = 0.0;
			double num12 = Math.Abs(flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value - unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value);
			if (num12 > flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value || num12 > unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value)
			{
				base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(unknownStream, FlashType.PHFlash, flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value, unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value);
			}
			while (num10 < length)
			{
				double num13 = 0.0;
				double num14 = 1E+20;
				double num15 = 0.0;
				double maxDH = 1E+20;
				if (base.HeatFlux != 0.0)
				{
					maxDH = PipeFlowFunctions.GetMaximumEnthalpyChange(flowStream, base.ExternalTemperature, base.PhysicalPropertyPackage);
				}
				base.Correlation.FlowStream = flowStream;
				num8 = base.Correlation.CalculateEquivalentDensity();
				int num16 = 0;
				while (num14 > Math.Abs(base.Accuracy * num15) && (num15 == 0.0 || !(Math.Abs(num15) < base.Accuracy)) && num16 < maxLoopCounter())
				{
					num16++;
					base.Correlation.FlowStream = unknownStream;
					num9 = base.Correlation.CalculateEquivalentDensity();
					IFlowStream flowStream2 = (IFlowStream)knownStream.Clone();
					try
					{
						flowStream2.AvarageFlowStream(knownStream, unknownStream, 0.5, 1.0, overrideExistingData: true);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						double data = (knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value + unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value) / 2.0;
						double data2 = (knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value + unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value) / 2.0;
						base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(flowStream2, FlashType.PHFlash, data, data2);
					}
					base.Correlation.FlowStream = flowStream2;
					num2 = base.Correlation.CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
					switch (base.StaticHeadMethod)
					{
					case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Include:
						num3 = base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
						break;
					case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Ignore:
						num3 = 0.0;
						break;
					case StaticHeadContributionEnum.IgnoreDownhill:
						num3 = ((!(base.InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor >= 0.0)) ? 0.0 : base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient());
						break;
					}
					num4 = CalculateAccelerationGradient(unknownStream, flowStream, flowStream2, num9, num8, inMassFlux, outMassFlux, num11, StepwiseFactor, FlowDirectionFactor);
					num = num2 + num3 + num4;
					num5 = FlowDirectionFactor * num * num11;
					num6 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value + num5 * (0.0 - StepwiseFactor);
					if (num6 < 0.0)
					{
						num5 = 0.0;
						num6 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value + num5 * (0.0 - StepwiseFactor);
						if (base.Log != null)
						{
							base.Log.Add(MessageLogType.Error, Resources.NegativePressureContinueWithZeroDP);
						}
						throw new Exception(Resources.NegativePressure);
					}
					num7 = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
					if (base.CalculateEnthalpyChange)
					{
						num13 = CalculateDeltaEnthalpy(flowStream, unknownStream, StepwiseFactor, num11, maxDH, num8, num9, FlowDirectionFactor);
						num7 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value + StepwiseFactor * num13;
					}
					base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(unknownStream, FlashType.PHFlash, num6, num7);
					num14 = Math.Abs(num5 - num15);
					num15 = num5;
				}
				num10 += num11;
				mFrictionPressureDrop += (0.0 - num2) * FlowDirectionFactor * num11;
				mGravityPressureDrop += (0.0 - num3) * FlowDirectionFactor * num11;
				mAccelerationPressureDrop += (0.0 - num4) * FlowDirectionFactor * num11;
			}
			return true;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			if (base.Log != null)
			{
				base.Log.Add(MessageLogType.Error, string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "CalculateOutletStream " + Resources.WithLength));
			}
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "CalculateOutletStream " + Resources.WithLength), innerException);
		}
	}

	protected override int maxLoopCounter()
	{
		if (DoInnerLoop)
		{
			return base.MaxPresIter;
		}
		return 1;
	}
}

	public override bool CalculateUnknownFlowStream(IFlowStream unknownStream, IFlowStream knownStream, double length, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{

		double num14 = 0.0;
		double num15 = -32767.0;
		double num16 = -32767.0;
		IFlowStream flowStream = null;
		double num17 = -32767.0;
		double num18 = 0.0;
		double num19 = 0.0;
		double num20 = 0.0;
		if (!IsReady())
		{
			return false;
		}
