		{
			flowStream = (IFlowStream)knownStream.Clone();
			if (StepwiseFactor == 1.0)
			{
				SetMassFlowrates(unknownStream.MassFlowrate.Value, flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
			}
			else
			{
				SetMassFlowrates(flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value, unknownStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
			}
			double inMassFlux = InletMassFlux();
			double outMassFlux = OutletMassFlux();
			int num21 = 0;
			num19 = 0.0;
			num20 = 0.0;
			mFrictionPressureDrop = 0.0;
			mGravityPressureDrop = 0.0;
			mAccelerationPressureDrop = 0.0;
			double num22 = Math.Abs(flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value - unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value);
			if (num22 > flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value || num22 > unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value)
			{
				base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(unknownStream, FlashType.PHFlash, flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value, flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value);
			}
			while (num19 < length)
			{
				num21++;
				double num23 = 0.0;
				if (num19 == 0.0)
				{
					base.Correlation.FlowStream = flowStream;
					num12 = base.Correlation.CalculateEquivalentDensity();
					num4 = base.Correlation.CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
					switch (base.StaticHeadMethod)
					{
					case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Include:
						num6 = base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
						break;
					case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Ignore:
						num6 = 0.0;
						break;
					case StaticHeadContributionEnum.IgnoreDownhill:
						num6 = ((!(base.InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor >= 0.0)) ? 0.0 : base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient());
						break;
					}
					if (base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop == AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Include)
					{
						if (flowStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 || flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
						{
							base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop = AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Homogeneous;
							num8 = 0.0;
						}
						else
						{
							num8 = base.Correlation.CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						num8 = 0.0;
					}
					num2 = num4 + num6 + num8;
				}
				else
				{
					flowStream = (IFlowStream)unknownStream.Clone();
					num12 = num13;
					num2 = num3;
					num4 = num5;
					num6 = num7;
					num8 = num9;
				}
				num17 = 1E+20;
				num = 0.0;
				if (num13 <= 0.0)
				{
					num13 = num12;
				}
				int num24 = 0;
				while (num17 > base.Accuracy * length && num24 < maxLoopCounter())
				{
					num24++;
					int num25 = Math.Sign(FlowDirectionFactor * num2 * (num + 1E-05) - num23);
					num11 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value * (1.0 - StepwiseFactor * (double)num25 * PressureStepRatio);
					num10 = num11 - flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
					num18 = Math.Min(num14, length);
					if (base.HeatFlux != 0.0 && num > 0.0)
					{
						if (base.HeatFlux != RealValue.FEMPTY)
						{
							num15 = (num16 = base.HeatFlux);
						}
						else
						{
							double thermalResistance = base.ExternalThermalResistance + Conduction.ThermalResistanceForConvection(base.HeatTransferCoeff, Math.PI * base.InletDiameter);
							double thermalResistance2 = base.ExternalThermalResistance + Conduction.ThermalResistanceForConvection(base.HeatTransferCoeff, Math.PI * base.OutletDiameter);
							double num26 = Math.PI * base.InletDiameter;
							double num27 = Math.PI * base.OutletDiameter;
							if (StepwiseFactor == 1.0)
							{
								num15 = Conduction.HeatFlux(flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value, base.ExternalTemperature, thermalResistance2) / num27;
								num16 = Conduction.HeatFlux(unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value, base.ExternalTemperature, thermalResistance) / num26;
							}
							else
							{
								num15 = Conduction.HeatFlux(flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value, base.ExternalTemperature, thermalResistance) / num26;
								num16 = Conduction.HeatFlux(unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value, base.ExternalTemperature, thermalResistance2) / num27;
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						num15 = (num16 = 0.0);
					}
					double data = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
					if (base.CalculateEnthalpyChange)
					{
						double num28 = 0.0;
						double maxDH = 1E+20;
						if (num15 != 0.0 && num16 != 0.0)
						{
							maxDH = PipeFlowFunctions.GetMaximumEnthalpyChange(flowStream, base.ExternalTemperature, base.PhysicalPropertyPackage);
						}
						num28 = ((StepwiseFactor != 1.0) ? GetEnthalpyChange(flowStream, unknownStream, num12, num13, 0.0 - num15, 0.0 - num16, base.InletDiameter, base.OutletDiameter, num18, base.InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor, maxDH) : GetEnthalpyChange(unknownStream, flowStream, num13, num12, 0.0 - num16, 0.0 - num15, base.InletDiameter, base.OutletDiameter, num18, base.InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor, maxDH));
						data = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value + StepwiseFactor * num28;
					}
					base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(unknownStream, FlashType.PHFlash, num11, data);
					base.Correlation.FlowStream = unknownStream;
					num13 = base.Correlation.CalculateEquivalentDensity();
					switch (base.GradientEstimate)
					{
					case GradientEstimateMethod.UseKnownStream:
						num3 = num2;
						num5 = num4;
						num7 = num6;
						num9 = num8;
						break;
					case GradientEstimateMethod.UseAverage:
						num5 = base.Correlation.CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
						switch (base.StaticHeadMethod)
						{
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Include:
							num7 = base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
							break;
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Ignore:
							num7 = 0.0;
							break;
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.IgnoreDownhill:
							num7 = ((!(base.InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor >= 0.0)) ? 0.0 : base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient());
							break;
						}
						if (base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop == AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Include)
						{
							if (unknownStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 || unknownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
							{
								base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop = AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Homogeneous;
								num9 = 0.0;
								num2 -= num8;
								num8 = 0.0;
							}
							else
							{
								num9 = base.Correlation.CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
							}
						}
						else
						{
							num9 = 0.0;
						}
						num3 = num5 + num7 + num9;
						break;
					case GradientEstimateMethod.UseUnknownStream:
						num5 = base.Correlation.CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
						switch (base.StaticHeadMethod)
						{
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Include:
							num7 = base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
							break;
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.Ignore:
							num7 = 0.0;
							break;
						case StaticHeadContributionEnum.IgnoreDownhill:
							num7 = ((!(base.InclinationAngle * FlowDirectionFactor >= 0.0)) ? 0.0 : base.Correlation.CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient());
							break;
						}
						if (base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop == AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Include)
						{
							if (unknownStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 || unknownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
							{
								base.IncludeAccelerationPressureDrop = AccelerationPressureMethodEnum.Homogeneous;
								num9 = 0.0;
							}
							else
							{
								num9 = base.Correlation.CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
							}
						}
						else
						{
							num9 = 0.0;
						}
						num3 = num5 + num7 + num9;
						num2 = num3;
						num4 = num5;
						num6 = num7;
						num8 = num9;
						break;
					}
					num23 = CalculateAdditionalAccelerationPressureDrop(flowStream, unknownStream, num12, num13, inMassFlux, outMassFlux, StepwiseFactor, FlowDirectionFactor);
					num14 = Math.Abs(((0.0 - StepwiseFactor) * num10 + num23) / (FlowDirectionFactor * (0.5 * (num2 + num3))));
					num17 = Math.Abs(num14 - num);
					if (num24 > maxLoopCounter() || num21 > 1000)
					{
						if (base.Log != null)
						{
							base.Log.Add(MessageLogType.Error, Resources.FailedToSolveInIterationLimit);
						}
						throw new Exception("PipeCell.CalculateDPWithHTFSMethod " + Resources.FailedToSolveInIterationLimit);
					}
					if (num14 > length && PressureStepRatio >= 0.01)
					{
						PressureStepRatio *= 0.75;
					}
					else if (num14 < length / 100.0 || num14 < 5.0 * base.InletDiameter)
					{
						PressureStepRatio *= 1.25;
					}
					num = num14;
				}
				num19 += num;
				num20 += num18;
				double num29 = -0.5 * (num4 + num5) * FlowDirectionFactor * num;
				double num30 = -0.5 * (num6 + num7) * FlowDirectionFactor * num;
				double num31 = -0.5 * (num8 + num9) * FlowDirectionFactor * num + num23;
				if (num19 < length)
				{
					mFrictionPressureDrop += num29;
					mGravityPressureDrop += num30;
					mAccelerationPressureDrop += num31;
				}
				else
				{
					mFrictionPressureDrop += num29 * (1.0 - (num19 - length) / num);
					mGravityPressureDrop += num30 * (1.0 - (num19 - length) / num);
					mAccelerationPressureDrop += num31 * (1.0 - (num19 - length) / num);
				}
			}
			if (num19 - length > base.Accuracy * length)
			{
				num10 *= 1.0 - (num19 - length) / num;
				num11 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value + num10;
				if (num11 <= 0.0)
				{
					if (base.Log != null)
					{
						base.Log.Add(MessageLogType.Error, Resources.NegativePressure);
					}
					throw new Exception(Resources.NegativePressure);
				}
				double data2 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value + (unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value - flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value) * (1.0 - (num20 - length) / num18);
				base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(unknownStream, FlashType.PHFlash, num11, data2);
			}
			return true;
		}
		catch (Exception innerException)
		{
			if (base.Log != null)
			{
				base.Log.Add(MessageLogType.Error, string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "CalculateOutletStream " + Resources.WithPressure));
			}
			throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "CalculateOutletStream " + Resources.WithPressure), innerException);
		}
	}
}
public class CompressibleHTFSPressureSolver : PipeFlowPressureSolver
{
	private bool mUseIdealGasApprox;

	private bool mUseVenaContracta;

	public bool UseIdealGasApproximation
	{
		get
		{
			return mUseIdealGasApprox;
		}
		set
		{
			mUseIdealGasApprox = value;
		}
	}

	public bool UseVenaContracta
	{
		get
		{
			return mUseVenaContracta;
		}
		set
		{
			mUseVenaContracta = value;
		}
	}

	public CompressibleHTFSPressureSolver()
	{
		mUseIdealGasApprox = false;
		mUseVenaContracta = false;
	}

	public override bool IsReady()
	{
		if (base.InletDiameter <= 0.0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.OutletDiameter <= 0.0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.PhysicalPropertyPackage == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public override bool CalculateUnknownFlowStream(IFlowStream unknownStream, IFlowStream knownStream, double length, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{
		if (!IsReady())
		{
			return false;
		}
		IFlowStream flowStream = (IFlowStream)knownStream.Clone();
		if (StepwiseFactor == 1.0)
		{
			SetMassFlowrates(unknownStream.MassFlowrate.Value, flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
		}
		else
		{
			SetMassFlowrates(flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value, unknownStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
		}
		bool flag = true;
		flag = ((base.InletDiameter < base.OutletDiameter && FlowDirectionFactor == 1.0) ? ((!(StepwiseFactor > 0.0)) ? CompressibleExpansionCalculationForward(flowStream, unknownStream, base.InletDiameter, base.OutletDiameter, InletMassFlux(), UseIdealGasApproximation) : CompressibleExpansionCalculation(unknownStream, flowStream, base.InletDiameter, base.OutletDiameter, InletMassFlux(), UseIdealGasApproximation)) : ((!(StepwiseFactor > 0.0)) ? CompressibleContractionCalculationForward(flowStream, unknownStream, base.InletDiameter, base.OutletDiameter, InletMassFlux(), UseIdealGasApproximation, UseVenaContracta) : CompressibleContractionCalculation(unknownStream, flowStream, base.InletDiameter, base.OutletDiameter, InletMassFlux(), UseIdealGasApproximation, UseVenaContracta)));
		mFrictionPressureDrop = 0.0;
		mGravityPressureDrop = 0.0;
		mAccelerationPressureDrop = (unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value - knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value) * StepwiseFactor;
		return flag;
	}

	internal double IdealGasContractionFunction(double Pin, double Pout, IFlowStream inStream, IFlowStream outStream, double inDiameter, double outDiameter, double inMassFlux, double StepwiseFactor)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (inStream.NumberOfPhases < 2)
		{
			double x = Pout / Pin;
			double num = outStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Cp.Value / outStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Cv.Value;
			double value = outStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
			double num2 = inMassFlux * inDiameter * inDiameter / outDiameter / outDiameter;
			double num3 = Math.Pow(x, 2.0 / num) * inMassFlux * inMassFlux / 2.0;
			double num4 = (0.0 - num2) * num2 / 2.0;
			double num5 = (0.0 - num) * Pout * value / (num - 1.0);
			double num6 = num * Pout * value * Math.Pow(x, (1.0 - num) / num) / (num - 1.0);
			result = num3 + num4 + num5 + num6;
			double num7 = Math.Pow(x, 1.0 / num);
			double inDensity = value / num7;
			double enthalpyChange = GetEnthalpyChange(inStream, outStream, inDensity, value, 0.0, 0.0, inDiameter, outDiameter, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
			if (StepwiseFactor > 0.0)
			{
				double data = outStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value + enthalpyChange;
				base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(inStream, FlashType.PHFlash, Pin, data);
			}
			else
			{
				double data2 = inStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value - enthalpyChange;
				base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(outStream, FlashType.PHFlash, Pout, data2);
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal double IdealGasExpansionFunction(double Pin, double Pout, IFlowStream inStream, IFlowStream outStream, double inDiameter, double outDiameter, double inMassFlux, double StepwiseFactor)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (inStream.NumberOfPhases < 2)
		{
			if (StepwiseFactor > 0.0)
			{
				double num = outStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Cp.Value / outStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Cv.Value;
				double num2 = outDiameter * outDiameter / inDiameter / inDiameter;
				double value = outStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Density.Value;
				double num3 = inMassFlux * inMassFlux * (0.5 * (num - 1.0) / num - 1.0 / num2);
				double num4 = Pout * value + inMassFlux * inMassFlux / num2 / num2;
				double num5 = 0.0 - (Pout * value + (num - 1.0) * inMassFlux * inMassFlux / num / 2.0 / num2 / num2);
				double num6 = (0.0 - num4 + Math.Sqrt(num4 * num4 - 4.0 * num3 * num5)) / 2.0 / num3;
				if (num6 < 0.0)
				{
					num6 = 1.0;
				}
				double num7 = inMassFlux * inMassFlux * (1.0 / num2 - num6) / value / num2;
				Pin = Pout + num7;
				double inDensity = value / num6;
				double enthalpyChange = GetEnthalpyChange(inStream, outStream, inDensity, value, 0.0, 0.0, inDiameter, outDiameter, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
				double data = outStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value + enthalpyChange;
				base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(inStream, FlashType.PHFlash, Pin, data);
			}
			else
			{
				double num8 = inStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Cp.Value / inStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Cv.Value;
				double num9 = outDiameter * outDiameter / inDiameter / inDiameter;
				double value2 = inStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Density.Value;
				double num10 = (0.0 - (1.0 + inMassFlux * inMassFlux / 2.0 / num8 / Pin / value2)) / (1.0 + (num8 - 1.0) * inMassFlux * inMassFlux / 2.0 / num8 / Pin / value2);
				double num11 = inMassFlux * inMassFlux * (1.0 + (num8 - 1.0) * inMassFlux * inMassFlux / 2.0 / num8 / Pin) / num9 / Pin;
				double num12 = (0.0 - num10 + Math.Sqrt(num10 * num10 - 4.0 * num11)) / 2.0;
				if (num12 < 0.0)
				{
					num12 = 1.0;
				}
				double num13 = inMassFlux * inMassFlux * (num12 / num9 - 1.0) / value2 / num9;
				Pout = Pin - num13;
				double outDensity = value2 / num12;
				double enthalpyChange2 = GetEnthalpyChange(inStream, outStream, value2, outDensity, 0.0, 0.0, inDiameter, outDiameter, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
				double data2 = inStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value + enthalpyChange2;
				base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(outStream, FlashType.PHFlash, Pout, data2);
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal double ExpansionSolveFunction(double Pin, double Pout, IFlowStream inStream, IFlowStream outStream, double inDiameter, double outDiameter, double inMassFlux, double StepwiseFactor)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = outDiameter * outDiameter / inDiameter / inDiameter;
		if (StepwiseFactor > 0.0)
		{
			base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(inStream, FlashType.PHFlash, Pin, outStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value);
		}
		else
		{
			base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(outStream, FlashType.PHFlash, Pout, inStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value);
		}
		double num3 = 1.0 / inStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
		double num4 = 1.0 / outStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
		if (inStream.NumberOfPhases > 1 && inStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) != null)
		{
			double value = inStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double value2 = inStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Density.Value;
			double value3 = inStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).MassFraction.Value;
			num3 = TwoPhaseFlow.MomentumEquivVolume(value, value2, value3);
		}
		if (outStream.NumberOfPhases > 1 && outStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) != null)
		{
			double value4 = outStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double value5 = outStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Density.Value;
			double value6 = outStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).MassFraction.Value;
			num4 = TwoPhaseFlow.MomentumEquivVolume(value4, value5, value6);
		}
		double num5 = Pin - Pout;
		double num6 = inMassFlux * inMassFlux / num2 * (num4 / num2 - num3);
		return num5 - num6;
	}

	internal double ContractionSolveFunction(double Pin, double Pout, IFlowStream inStream, IFlowStream outStream, double inDiameter, double outDiameter, double inMassFlux, double StepwiseFactor)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double value = outStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
		double value2 = outStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value;
		if (StepwiseFactor < 0.0)
		{
			value = inStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
			value2 = inStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value;
			base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(outStream, FlashType.PHFlash, Pout, value);
		}
		double num2 = 0.0;
		double num3 = 1.0 / outStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
		if (outStream.NumberOfPhases > 1 && outStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) != null)
		{
			double value3 = outStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
			double value4 = outStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Density.Value;
			double value5 = outStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).MassFraction.Value;
			num3 = TwoPhaseFlow.MomentumEquivVolume(value3, value4, value5);
		}
		double num4 = num3;
		if (Pin != Pout)
		{
			if (StepwiseFactor > 0.0)
			{
				base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(inStream, FlashType.PHFlash, Pin, value);
			}
			num4 = 1.0 / inStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
			if (inStream.NumberOfPhases > 1 && inStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) != null)
			{
				double value6 = inStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
				double value7 = inStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Density.Value;
				double value8 = inStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).MassFraction.Value;
				num4 = TwoPhaseFlow.MomentumEquivVolume(value6, value7, value8);
			}
			bool flag = false;
			int num5 = 2;
			double[] array = new double[num5];
			array[0] = num4;
			array[1] = num3;
			double num6 = MathLib.SimpsonIntegral(Pin, Pout, array);
			num5 = 3;
			IFlowStream flowStream = (IFlowStream)inStream.Clone();
			while (!flag && num5 <= 9)
			{
				double num7 = (Pin - Pout) / (double)(num5 - 1);
				double[] array2 = new double[num5];
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					array2[2 * i] = array[i];
				}
				for (int j = 1; j < num5; j += 2)
				{
					double data = Pin - num7 * (double)j;
					base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(flowStream, FlashType.PHFlash, data, value);
					double num8 = 1.0 / flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
					if (flowStream.NumberOfPhases > 1 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) != null)
					{
						double value9 = flowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
						double value10 = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Density.Value;
						double value11 = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).MassFraction.Value;
						num8 = TwoPhaseFlow.MomentumEquivVolume(value9, value10, value11);
					}
					array2[j] = num8;
				}
				num2 = MathLib.SimpsonIntegral(Pin, Pout, array2);
				if (num6 != 0.0 && Math.Abs((num2 - num6) / num6) < 0.05)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				num6 = num2;
				array = array2;
				num5 = 2 * num5 - 1;
			}
		}
		double num9 = inMassFlux * inDiameter * inDiameter / outDiameter / outDiameter;
		double num10 = 0.5 * (inMassFlux * inMassFlux * num4 * num4 - num9 * num9 * num3 * num3);
		return num10 - num2;
	}

	internal bool CompressibleContractionCalculation(IFlowStream inStream, IFlowStream outStream, double inDiameter, double outDiameter, double inMassFlux, bool useIdealGas, bool useVenaContracta)
	{
		bool flag = true;
		IFlowStream interOutStream = (IFlowStream)outStream.Clone();
		double interOutDiameter = outDiameter;
		if (useVenaContracta)
		{
			double num = SinglePhaseFlow.ContractionCoeff(outDiameter / inDiameter);
			interOutDiameter = outDiameter * Math.Sqrt(num);
			double num2 = inMassFlux * inDiameter * inDiameter / outDiameter / outDiameter;
			double inMassFlux2 = num2 / num;
			flag = CompressibleExpansionCalculation(interOutStream, outStream, interOutDiameter, outDiameter, inMassFlux2, useIdealGas);
			if (!flag)
			{
				return flag;
			}
		}
		double pout = interOutStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
		UnaryFunction func = (double p) => (useIdealGas && interOutStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 && interOutStream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour) ? IdealGasContractionFunction(p, pout, inStream, interOutStream, inDiameter, interOutDiameter, inMassFlux, 1.0) : ContractionSolveFunction(p, pout, inStream, interOutStream, inDiameter, interOutDiameter, inMassFlux, 1.0);
		double xmax = pout * 1.1;
		SecantRootFinder secantRootFinder = new SecantRootFinder();
		double num3 = secantRootFinder.FindRoot(pout, xmax, func, BracketExpandType.IncreaseMaximum);
		if (num3 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			flag = false;
		}
		return flag;
	}

	internal bool CompressibleContractionCalculationForward(IFlowStream inStream, IFlowStream outStream, double inDiameter, double outDiameter, double inMassFlux, bool useIdealGas, bool useVenaContracta)
	{
		bool flag = true;
		IFlowStream interOutStream = (IFlowStream)inStream.Clone();
		double interOutDiameter = outDiameter;
		if (useVenaContracta)
		{
			double d = SinglePhaseFlow.ContractionCoeff(outDiameter / inDiameter);
			interOutDiameter = outDiameter * Math.Sqrt(d);
		}
		double pin = inStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
		UnaryFunction func = (double p) => (useIdealGas && interOutStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 && interOutStream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour) ? IdealGasContractionFunction(pin, p, inStream, interOutStream, inDiameter, interOutDiameter, inMassFlux, -1.0) : ContractionSolveFunction(pin, p, inStream, interOutStream, inDiameter, interOutDiameter, inMassFlux, -1.0);
		double xmin = pin * 0.9;
		SecantRootFinder secantRootFinder = new SecantRootFinder();
		double num = secantRootFinder.FindRoot(xmin, pin, func, BracketExpandType.DecreaseMinimum);
		if (num == RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			flag = false;
		}
		if (useVenaContracta && flag)
		{
			double num2 = SinglePhaseFlow.ContractionCoeff(outDiameter / inDiameter);
			interOutDiameter = outDiameter * Math.Sqrt(num2);
			double num3 = inMassFlux * inDiameter * inDiameter / outDiameter / outDiameter;
			double inMassFlux2 = num3 / num2;
			flag = CompressibleExpansionCalculationForward(interOutStream, outStream, interOutDiameter, outDiameter, inMassFlux2, useIdealGas);
		}
		else if (!useVenaContracta && flag)
		{
			base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(outStream, FlashType.PTFlash, interOutStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value, interOutStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value);
		}
		return flag;
	}

	internal bool CompressibleExpansionCalculation(IFlowStream inStream, IFlowStream outStream, double inDiameter, double outDiameter, double inMassFlux, bool useIdealGas)
	{
		bool result = true;
		double pout = outStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
		UnaryFunction func = (double p) => (useIdealGas && outStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 && outStream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour) ? IdealGasExpansionFunction(p, pout, inStream, outStream, inDiameter, outDiameter, inMassFlux, 1.0) : ExpansionSolveFunction(p, pout, inStream, outStream, inDiameter, outDiameter, inMassFlux, 1.0);
		SecantRootFinder secantRootFinder = new SecantRootFinder();
		double num = secantRootFinder.FindRoot(pout * 0.9, pout, func, BracketExpandType.DecreaseMinimum);
		if (num == RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			num = secantRootFinder.FindRoot(pout, 1.1 * pout, func, BracketExpandType.IncreaseMaximum);
		}
		double chockedPressure = HTFSUtility.GetChockedPressure(outStream, base.PhysicalPropertyPackage, inDiameter);
		if (num == RealValue.FEMPTY && chockedPressure == RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			result = false;
		}
		else if (num == RealValue.FEMPTY || num < chockedPressure)
		{
			base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(inStream, FlashType.PHFlash, chockedPressure, outStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value);
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal bool CompressibleExpansionCalculationForward(IFlowStream inStream, IFlowStream outStream, double inDiameter, double outDiameter, double inMassFlux, bool useIdealGas)
	{
		bool result = true;
		double pin = inStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
		UnaryFunction func = (double p) => (useIdealGas && outStream.NumberOfPhases < 2 && outStream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour) ? IdealGasExpansionFunction(pin, p, inStream, outStream, inDiameter, outDiameter, inMassFlux, -1.0) : ExpansionSolveFunction(pin, p, inStream, outStream, inDiameter, outDiameter, inMassFlux, -1.0);
		SecantRootFinder secantRootFinder = new SecantRootFinder();
		double num = secantRootFinder.FindRoot(pin, 1.1 * pin, func, BracketExpandType.IncreaseMaximum);
		if (num == RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			num = secantRootFinder.FindRoot(0.9 * pin, pin, func, BracketExpandType.DecreaseMinimum);
		}
		if (num == RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			result = false;
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal void FlashStreamWithConstantStagnationEnthalpy(IFlowStream tStream, double P, double stagEnth, double massFlux, double laccuracy, int maxIterate)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		int num2 = 0;
		bool flag = false;
		while (!flag && num2 < maxIterate)
		{
			double num3 = ((tStream.NumberOfPhases >= 2) ? TwoPhaseFlow.MomentumEnergyOfMixture(massFlux, tStream.GetPhaseByID(1).MassFraction.Value, tStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value, tStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Density.Value) : SinglePhaseFlow.MomentumEnergy(massFlux, tStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Density.Value));
			double data = stagEnth - num3;
			base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(tStream, FlashType.PHFlash, P, data);
			if (Math.Abs((num - num3) / num3) < laccuracy)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			num = num3;
			num2++;
		}
	}
}
public abstract class GasPressureSolver : PipeFlowPressureSolver
{
	protected double mPguess;

	protected double mRoughness;

	private bool mUseAdjustedDensity;

	protected FrictionFactorMethodEnum mFrictionFactorMethod;

	protected double mFittingCoeffA;

	protected double mFittingCoeffB;

	protected double mFittingRoughness;

	protected double mLengthMultiplier;

	protected double mFrictionFactor;

	protected double mEquivLength;

	protected double mRe;

	public double PressureGuess
	{
		set
		{
			mPguess = value;
		}
	}

	public double PipeRoughness
	{
		set
		{
			mRoughness = value;
		}
	}

	public bool UseAdjustedDensity
	{
		get
		{
			return mUseAdjustedDensity;
		}
		set
		{
			mUseAdjustedDensity = value;
		}
	}

	public FrictionFactorMethodEnum FrictionFactorMethod
	{
		set
		{
			mFrictionFactorMethod = value;
		}
	}

	public double FittingRoughness
	{
		set
		{
			mFittingRoughness = value;
		}
	}

	public double FittingCoeffA
	{
		set
		{
			mFittingCoeffA = value;
		}
	}

	public double FittingCoeffB
	{
		set
		{
			mFittingCoeffB = value;
		}
	}

	public double PipeLengthMultiplier
	{
		set
		{
			mLengthMultiplier = value;
		}
	}

	public double UsedFrictionFactor => mFrictionFactor;

	public double EquivalentPipeLength => mEquivLength;

	public double UsedReynoldsNumber => mRe;

	public GasPressureSolver()
	{
		mPguess = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		mRoughness = 0.0;
		mUseAdjustedDensity = true;
		mFrictionFactorMethod = FrictionFactorMethodEnum.Chen;
		mFrictionFactor = 0.0;
		mFittingCoeffA = 0.0;
		mFittingCoeffB = 0.0;
		mFittingRoughness = 0.0;
		mLengthMultiplier = 1.0;
		mRe = RealValue.FEMPTY;
	}

	internal double AdjustedIdealGasDensity(IFlowStream strm, double pref, double p)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 0.0;
		IPhase phaseByID = strm.GetPhaseByID(0);
		IPhase phaseByType = strm.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		IPhase combinedLiquidPhase = strm.CombinedLiquidPhase;
		double num3 = Math.Abs(strm.MassFlowrate.Value);
		double result = phaseByID.Density.Value;
		if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			num2 = num3 / phaseByID.MolecularWeight.Value * phaseByID.MolarVapourFraction.Value * phaseByType.MolecularWeight.Value / phaseByType.Density.Value;
			num2 *= pref / p;
		}
		if (combinedLiquidPhase != null)
		{
			double num4 = 1.0 - phaseByID.MolarVapourFraction.Value;
			num = num3 / phaseByID.MolecularWeight.Value * num4 * combinedLiquidPhase.MolecularWeight.Value / combinedLiquidPhase.Density.Value;
		}
		if (num2 != 0.0 || num != 0.0)
		{
			result = num3 / (num2 + num);
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal bool IsSingleGasPhase(IFlowStream stream)
	{
		bool result = false;
		if (stream.NumberOfPhases == 1 && stream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
		{
			result = true;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public override bool IsReady()
	{
		if (base.InletDiameter <= 0.0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.OutletDiameter <= 0.0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (base.PhysicalPropertyPackage == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public double FrictionFactor(double Re, double ed)
	{
		return PipeFlowFunctions.FrictionFactor(mFrictionFactorMethod, Re, ed);
	}
}
public class IsothermalGasPressureSolver : GasPressureSolver
{
	public override bool CalculateUnknownFlowStream(IFlowStream unknownStream, IFlowStream knownStream, double length, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{
		if (!IsReady())
		{
			return false;
		}
		double value = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
		double num = ((knownStream.NumberOfPhases < 2) ? knownStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Viscosity.Value : ((knownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) != null) ? ViscosityCalculator.VolumeWeightedViscosity(knownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour), knownStream.CombinedLiquidPhase) : knownStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value));
		double value2 = knownStream.MassFlowrate.Value;
		double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * base.InletDiameter * base.InletDiameter;
		double num3 = value2 / value / num2;
		double num4 = num2 * num2;
		double num5 = value2 * value2;
		double num6 = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
		double num7 = num6;
		double value3 = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
		double num8 = ((mPguess != RealValue.FEMPTY) ? mPguess : ((num7 != value3) ? num7 : ((!(StepwiseFactor > 0.0)) ? (0.5 * value3) : (1.5 * value3))));
		for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++)
		{
			double num9 = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
			if (base.UseAdjustedDensity)
			{
				num9 = AdjustedIdealGasDensity(unknownStream, num7, num8);
			}
			double num10 = value2 / num9 / num2;
			double num11 = ((unknownStream.NumberOfPhases < 2) ? unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Viscosity.Value : ((unknownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) != null) ? ViscosityCalculator.VolumeWeightedViscosity(unknownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour), unknownStream.CombinedLiquidPhase) : unknownStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value));
			double num12 = 0.5 * (value + num9);
			double num13 = 1.0 / num12;
			double num14 = 0.5 * (num + num11);
			double num15 = 0.5 * (num3 + num10);
			mRe = num12 * num15 * base.InletDiameter / num14;
			mFrictionFactor = FrictionFactor(mRe, mRoughness / base.InletDiameter);
			double num16 = PipeFlowFunctions.FittingLength(mFittingCoeffA, mFittingCoeffB, base.InletDiameter, mFittingRoughness, mFrictionFactor, mFrictionFactorMethod);
			mEquivLength = length * mLengthMultiplier + num16;
			double num17 = num5 / num4;
			double num18 = StepwiseFactor * num17 * Math.Log(num8 / value3);
			double num19 = 2.0 * mFrictionFactor * mEquivLength * num17 / base.InletDiameter;
			num6 = value3 + (num18 + num19) * num13 * StepwiseFactor;
			if (Math.Abs((num6 - num8) / num6) <= base.Accuracy)
			{
				break;
			}
			num8 = ((!(num6 > 60000000.0)) ? (((!(num6 < value3) || !(StepwiseFactor > 0.0)) && (!(num6 > value3) || !(StepwiseFactor < 0.0))) ? num6 : ((value3 + num8) / 2.0)) : ((60000000.0 + num8) / 2.0));
		}
		double value4 = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
		base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(unknownStream, FlashType.PTFlash, num6, value4);
		return true;
	}
}
public class AdiabaticGasPressureSolver : GasPressureSolver
{
	public override bool CalculateUnknownFlowStream(IFlowStream unknownStream, IFlowStream knownStream, double length, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{
		if (!IsReady())
		{
			return false;
		}
		double massFlow = knownStream.MassFlowrate.Value;
		double pipeArea = Math.PI / 4.0 * base.InletDiameter * base.InletDiameter;
		double value = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
		double knownDensity = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
		double num = ((knownStream.NumberOfPhases < 2) ? knownStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Viscosity.Value : ((knownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) != null) ? ViscosityCalculator.VolumeWeightedViscosity(knownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour), knownStream.CombinedLiquidPhase) : knownStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value));
		double knownVel = massFlow / knownDensity / pipeArea;
		double value2 = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Cp.Value;
		double num2 = value2 / (value2 - 8.3142);
		double num3 = ((unknownStream.NumberOfPhases < 2) ? unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Viscosity.Value : ((unknownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) != null) ? ViscosityCalculator.VolumeWeightedViscosity(unknownStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour), unknownStream.CombinedLiquidPhase) : unknownStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value));
		double value3 = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Cp.Value;
		double num4 = value3 / (value3 - 8.3142);
		double pRef = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
		double cpcv = 0.5 * (num4 + num2);
		double viav = 0.5 * (num + num3);
		UnaryFunction func = delegate(double p)
		{
			double num9 = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
			if (base.UseAdjustedDensity)
			{
				num9 = AdjustedIdealGasDensity(unknownStream, pRef, p);
			}
			double num10 = massFlow / num9 / pipeArea;
			double num11 = 0.5 * (knownDensity + num9);
			double num12 = 0.5 * (knownVel + num10);
			mRe = num11 * num12 * base.InletDiameter / viav;
			mFrictionFactor = FrictionFactor(mRe, mRoughness / base.InletDiameter);
			double num13 = PipeFlowFunctions.FittingLength(mFittingCoeffA, mFittingCoeffB, base.InletDiameter, mFittingRoughness, mFrictionFactor, mFrictionFactorMethod);
			mEquivLength = mLengthMultiplier * length + num13;
			double ld = mEquivLength / base.InletDiameter * StepwiseFactor;
			return AdiabaticGasFunction(p, num9, knownDensity, cpcv, massFlow / pipeArea, ld, mFrictionFactor);
		};
		IsothermalGasPressureSolver isothermalGasPressureSolver = new IsothermalGasPressureSolver();
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.PipeRoughness = mRoughness;
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.Accuracy = base.Accuracy;
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.SetGeometricalParameters(base.InletDiameter, base.InletDiameter, base.InclinationAngle);
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.PhysicalPropertyPackage = base.PhysicalPropertyPackage;
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.FittingCoeffA = mFittingCoeffA;
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.FittingCoeffB = mFittingCoeffB;
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.FittingRoughness = mFittingRoughness;
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.PipeLengthMultiplier = mLengthMultiplier;
		IFlowStream flowStream = (IFlowStream)unknownStream.Clone();
		bool flag = isothermalGasPressureSolver.CalculateUnknownFlowStream(flowStream, knownStream, length, StepwiseFactor, FlowDirectionFactor);
		double num5 = value + 0.2 * (flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value - value);
		double num6 = value + 2.0 * (flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value - value);
		if (num6 < num5)
		{
			double num7 = num5;
			num5 = num6;
			num6 = num7;
		}
		SecantRootFinder secantRootFinder = new SecantRootFinder();
		double num8 = secantRootFinder.FindRoot(num5, num6, func, BracketExpandType.ChangeBoth);
		if (num8 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			flag = false;
		}
		if (flag)
		{
			SetMassFlowrates(massFlow, massFlow);
			double inDensity = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
			if (base.UseAdjustedDensity)
			{
				inDensity = AdjustedIdealGasDensity(knownStream, value, num8);
			}
			double enthalpyChange = GetEnthalpyChange(unknownStream, knownStream, inDensity, knownDensity, 0.0, 0.0, base.InletDiameter, base.OutletDiameter, length, 0.0, 0.0);
			double data = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value + enthalpyChange;
			base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(unknownStream, FlashType.PHFlash, num8, data);
		}
		return flag;
	}

	internal bool CalculateUnknownStreamWithBisection(IFlowStream unknownStream, IFlowStream knownStream, double length, double StepwiseFactor, double FlowDirectionFactor)
	{
		if (!IsReady())
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!IsSingleGasPhase(knownStream))
		{
			return false;
		}
		double value = knownStream.MassFlowrate.Value;
		double num = Math.PI / 4.0 * base.InletDiameter * base.InletDiameter;
		double ld = length / base.InletDiameter;
		double value2 = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
		double value3 = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
		double value4 = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Viscosity.Value;
		double num2 = value / value3 / num;
		double value5 = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Cp.Value;
		double num3 = value5 / (value5 - 8.3142);
		double value6 = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Viscosity.Value;
		double value7 = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Cp.Value;
		double num4 = value7 / (value7 - 8.3142);
		double cpcv = 0.5 * (num4 + num3);
		double num5 = 0.5 * (value4 + value6);
		IsothermalGasPressureSolver isothermalGasPressureSolver = new IsothermalGasPressureSolver();
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.PipeRoughness = mRoughness;
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.Accuracy = base.Accuracy;
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.SetGeometricalParameters(base.InletDiameter, base.InletDiameter, base.InclinationAngle);
		isothermalGasPressureSolver.PhysicalPropertyPackage = base.PhysicalPropertyPackage;
		IFlowStream flowStream = (IFlowStream)unknownStream.Clone();
		bool flag = isothermalGasPressureSolver.CalculateUnknownFlowStream(flowStream, knownStream, length, StepwiseFactor, FlowDirectionFactor);
		double num6 = value2 + 0.2 * (flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value - value2);
		double num7 = unknownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
		if (base.UseAdjustedDensity)
		{
			num7 = AdjustedIdealGasDensity(unknownStream, value2, num6);
		}
		double num8 = value / num7 / num;
		double num9 = 0.5 * (value3 + num7);
		double num10 = 0.5 * (num2 + num8);
		double re = num9 * num10 * base.InletDiameter / num5;
		double ff = FrictionFactor(re, mRoughness / base.InletDiameter);
		double num11 = AdiabaticGasFunction(num6, num7, value3, cpcv, value / num, ld, ff);
		double num12 = value2 + 2.0 * (flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value - value2);
		num7 = AdjustedIdealGasDensity(knownStream, value2, num12);
		num8 = value / num7 / num;
		num9 = 0.5 * (value3 + num7);
		num10 = 0.5 * (num2 + num8);
		re = num9 * num10 * base.InletDiameter / num5;
		ff = FrictionFactor(re, mRoughness / base.InletDiameter);
		double num13 = AdiabaticGasFunction(num12, num7, value3, cpcv, value / num, ld, ff);
		double num14 = 0.0;
		bool flag2 = false;
		if (num11 * num13 < 0.0)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++)
			{
				num14 = 0.5 * (num6 + num12);
				num7 = AdjustedIdealGasDensity(knownStream, value2, num14);
				num8 = value / num7 / num;
				num9 = 0.5 * (value3 + num7);
				num10 = 0.5 * (num2 + num8);
				re = num9 * num10 * base.InletDiameter / num5;
				ff = FrictionFactor(re, mRoughness / base.InletDiameter);
				double num15 = AdiabaticGasFunction(num14, num7, value3, cpcv, value / num, ld, ff);
				if (num15 * num11 < 0.0)
				{
					num12 = num14;
					num13 = num15;
				}
				else if (num15 * num11 > 0.0)
				{
					num6 = num14;
					num11 = num15;
				}
				if (Math.Abs((num12 - num6) / num14) < base.Accuracy || num15 == 0.0)
				{
					flag2 = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (flag2)
		{
			double data = num14;
			SetMassFlowrates(value, value);
			double enthalpyChange = GetEnthalpyChange(unknownStream, knownStream, num7, value3, 0.0, 0.0, base.InletDiameter, base.OutletDiameter, length, 0.0, 0.0);
			double data2 = knownStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value + enthalpyChange;
			base.PhysicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(unknownStream, FlashType.PHFlash, data, data2);
		}
		return flag2;
	}

	internal double AdiabaticGasFunction(double p, double density1, double density2, double cpcv, double massflux, double ld, double ff)
	{
		double num = 1.0 / density1;
		double num2 = 1.0 / density2;
		double num3 = 4.0 * ff * ld;
		double num4 = (cpcv - 1.0) / (2.0 * cpcv);
		double num5 = 1.0 - num / num2 * (num / num2);
		double num6 = (cpcv + 1.0) / cpcv * Math.Log(num2 / num);
		return num3 - (num4 + p / num / massflux / massflux) * num5 + num6;
	}
}
public abstract class SwageFrictionFactorCorrelation : ICorrelationManager, IFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private IFlowStream mStream;

	private double mInDiameter;

	private double mOutDiameter;

	private double mAngle;

	protected ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation;

	public double InletDiameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mInDiameter;
		}
		set
		{
			mInDiameter = value;
		}
	}

	public double OutletDiameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mOutDiameter;
		}
		set
		{
			mOutDiameter = value;
		}
	}

	public double Angle
	{
		get
		{
			return mAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation LiquidHoldUpCorrelation
	{
		get
		{
			return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation;
		}
		set
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = value;
		}
	}

	public double CalculatedKFactor { get; private set; } = RealValue.FEMPTY;

	public IFlowStream FlowStream
	{
		get
		{
			return mStream;
		}
		set
		{
			mStream = value;
			if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation is PipeFlowCorrelation)
			{
				(mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation as PipeFlowCorrelation).FlowStream = mStream;
			}
		}
	}

	public SwageFrictionFactorCorrelation()
	{
		mStream = null;
		mInDiameter = 0.0;
		mOutDiameter = 0.0;
		mAngle = 0.0;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = null;
	}

	public SwageFrictionFactorCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double inDiam, double outDiam, double angle)
	{
		mStream = inStream;
		mInDiameter = inDiam;
		mOutDiameter = outDiam;
		mAngle = angle;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = null;
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient()
	{
		double num = CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
		double num3 = CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
		return num + num2 + num3;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (mStream != null && mInDiameter > 0.0 && mOutDiameter > 0.0)
		{
			double num = CalculateKFactor();
			double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(Math.Min(mInDiameter, mOutDiameter), 2.0);
			double num3 = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num2;
			result = -0.5 * num * num3 * num3 / CalculateEquivalentDensity();
			CalculatedKFactor = num;
		}
		else
		{
			CalculatedKFactor = 0.0;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity()
	{
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation == null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		}
		return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity()
	{
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation == null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		}
		return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public bool PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return false;
	}

	public abstract double CalculateKFactor();
}
public class EnlargementCraneFrictionCorrelation : SwageFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	public EnlargementCraneFrictionCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public EnlargementCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double inDiam, double outDiam, double angle)
		: base(inStream, inDiam, outDiam, angle)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, inDiam, 0.0, 0.0);
	}

	public EnlargementCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double inDiam, double outDiam, double angle, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, inDiam, outDiam, angle)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.OutletDiameter > 0.0)
		{
			double num = base.InletDiameter / base.OutletDiameter;
			result = ((!(base.Angle <= 45.0)) ? Crane.KFactorFormula4(num, 0.0, HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.Angle)) : Crane.KFactorFormula3(num, 0.0, HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.Angle)));
			result *= Math.Pow(num, 4.0);
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class EnlargementHTFSFrictionCorrelation : SwageFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	public EnlargementHTFSFrictionCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public EnlargementHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double inDiam, double outDiam, double angle)
		: base(inStream, inDiam, outDiam, angle)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, inDiam, 0.0, 0.0, HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.FittingType.Expansion);
	}

	public EnlargementHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double inDiam, double outDiam, double angle, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, inDiam, outDiam, angle)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.OutletDiameter > 0.0 && base.FlowStream != null)
		{
			double smallToLargeDiameterRatio = base.InletDiameter / base.OutletDiameter;
			result = ((!(base.Angle <= 45.0)) ? SinglePhaseFlow.KFactorForSuddenEnlargement(smallToLargeDiameterRatio) : SinglePhaseFlow.KFactorForGradualEnlargement(smallToLargeDiameterRatio, base.Angle));
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class ContractionCraneFrictionCorrelation : SwageFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	public ContractionCraneFrictionCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public ContractionCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double inDiam, double outDiam, double angle)
		: base(inStream, inDiam, outDiam, angle)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, inDiam, 0.0, 0.0);
	}

	public ContractionCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double inDiam, double outDiam, double angle, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, inDiam, outDiam, angle)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.InletDiameter > 0.0)
		{
			double num = base.OutletDiameter / base.InletDiameter;
			result = ((!(base.Angle <= 45.0)) ? Crane.KFactorFormula2(num, 0.0, HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.Angle)) : Crane.KFactorFormula1(num, 0.0, HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.Angle)));
			result *= Math.Pow(num, 4.0);
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class ContractionHTFSFrictionCorrelation : SwageFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	public ContractionHTFSFrictionCorrelation()
	{
	}

	public ContractionHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double inDiam, double outDiam, double angle)
		: base(inStream, inDiam, outDiam, angle)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, inDiam, 0.0, 0.0, HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.FittingType.Contraction);
	}

	public ContractionHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double inDiam, double outDiam, double angle, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, inDiam, outDiam, angle)
	{
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.InletDiameter > 0.0)
		{
			double smallToLargeDiameterRatio = base.OutletDiameter / base.InletDiameter;
			result = ((!(base.Angle < 180.0)) ? SinglePhaseFlow.KFactorForSuddenContraction(smallToLargeDiameterRatio) : SinglePhaseFlow.KFactorForGradualContraction(smallToLargeDiameterRatio, base.Angle));
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public abstract class TeePressureDropCorrelation : ICorrelationManager, IFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	protected IFlowStream mMainInStream;

	protected IFlowStream mMainOutStream;

	protected IFlowStream mBranchStream;

	protected IFlowStream mActiveStream;

	protected double mBodyDiameter;

	protected double mBranchDiameter;

	protected double mSideAngle;

	protected TeeType mTeeType;

	public double CalculatedKFactor { get; private set; } = RealValue.FEMPTY;

	public IFlowStream MainInletStream
	{
		get
		{
			return mMainInStream;
		}
		set
		{
			mMainInStream = value;
		}
	}

	public IFlowStream MainOutletStream
	{
		get
		{
			return mMainOutStream;
		}
		set
		{
			mMainOutStream = value;
		}
	}

	public IFlowStream BranchStream
	{
		get
		{
			return mBranchStream;
		}
		set
		{
			mBranchStream = value;
		}
	}

	public double BodyDiameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mBodyDiameter;
		}
		set
		{
			mBodyDiameter = value;
		}
	}

	public double BranchDiameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mBranchDiameter;
		}
		set
		{
			mBranchDiameter = value;
		}
	}

	public double SideAngle
	{
		get
		{
			return mSideAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mSideAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public TeeType TypeOfTee
	{
		get
		{
			return mTeeType;
		}
		set
		{
			mTeeType = value;
		}
	}

	public IFlowStream FlowStream
	{
		set
		{
			mActiveStream = value;
		}
	}

	public TeePressureDropCorrelation()
	{
		mMainInStream = null;
		mMainOutStream = null;
		mBranchStream = null;
		mBodyDiameter = 0.0;
		mBranchDiameter = 0.0;
		mSideAngle = 0.0;
		mTeeType = TeeType.Unknown;
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient()
	{
		double num = CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
		double num3 = CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
		return num + num2 + num3;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		IFlowStream streamForDP = GetStreamForDP();
		if (streamForDP != null)
		{
			double num = CalculateKFactor();
			double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(BodyDiameter, 2.0);
			double num3 = streamForDP.MassFlowrate.Value / num2;
			ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(streamForDP, BodyDiameter, 0.0, 0.0);
			double num4 = liquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
			result = -0.5 * num * num3 * num3 / num4;
			CalculatedKFactor = num;
		}
		else
		{
			CalculatedKFactor = 0.0;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity()
	{
		ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mActiveStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		return liquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity()
	{
		ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mActiveStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		return liquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public virtual bool PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return false;
	}

	internal abstract IFlowStream GetStreamForDP();

	internal double CalculateVolumeFlow(IFlowStream stream, double diameter)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (stream != null && stream.MassFlowrate.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(stream, diameter, 0.0, 0.0);
			num = liquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
			if (num > 0.0)
			{
				result = stream.MassFlowrate.Value / num;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal TeeAngle ConvertAngle(double angle)
	{
		TeeAngle result = TeeAngle._30;
		if (angle > 37.5)
		{
			result = TeeAngle._45;
		}
		if (angle > 52.5)
		{
			result = TeeAngle._60;
		}
		if (angle > 75.0)
		{
			result = TeeAngle._90;
		}
		if (angle > 105.0)
		{
			result = TeeAngle._120;
		}
		if (angle > 127.5)
		{
			result = TeeAngle._135;
		}
		if (angle > 142.5)
		{
			result = TeeAngle._150;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public abstract double CalculateKFactor();
}
public abstract class MillerDPCorrelation : TeePressureDropCorrelation
{
	private bool mIsAreaLimited;

	private MillerChartExtrapolationType mExtrapMethod;

	protected const double MIN_AREA_RATIO = 0.1;

	public bool AreaLimited
	{
		get
		{
			return mIsAreaLimited;
		}
		set
		{
			mIsAreaLimited = value;
		}
	}

	public MillerChartExtrapolationType ExtrapolationMethod
	{
		get
		{
			return mExtrapMethod;
		}
		set
		{
			mExtrapMethod = value;
		}
	}

	public MillerDPCorrelation()
	{
		mIsAreaLimited = false;
		mExtrapMethod = MillerChartExtrapolationType.None;
	}

	internal override IFlowStream GetStreamForDP()
	{
		IFlowStream result = null;
		if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Combiner)
		{
			result = base.MainOutletStream;
		}
		else if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Divider)
		{
			result = base.MainInletStream;
		}
		else if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.YCombiner || base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.YDivider)
		{
			result = base.BranchStream;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 0.0;
		if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.YCombiner || base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.YDivider)
		{
			result = 1.0;
		}
		else
		{
			if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Combiner)
			{
				num = base.BodyDiameter;
				num2 = CalculateVolumeFlow(base.MainOutletStream, base.BodyDiameter);
			}
			else if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Divider)
			{
				num = base.BodyDiameter;
				num2 = CalculateVolumeFlow(base.MainInletStream, base.BodyDiameter);
			}
			if (num != 0.0 && num2 != 0.0)
			{
				double val = base.BranchDiameter * base.BranchDiameter / num / num;
				val = Math.Min(val, 1.0);
				double num3 = CalculateVolumeFlow(base.BranchStream, base.BranchDiameter);
				double flowRatio = num3 / num2;
				result = GetMillerChartK(AreaLimited, val, flowRatio, base.SideAngle, base.TypeOfTee);
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal abstract double GetMillerChartK(bool AreaLimited, double areaRatio, double flowRatio, double branchAngle, TeeType TypeOfTee);
}
public class MillerMainMainDPCorrelation : MillerDPCorrelation
{
	internal override double GetMillerChartK(bool AreaLimited, double areaRatio, double flowRatio, double branchAngle, TeeType TypeOfTee)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = areaRatio;
		if (AreaLimited && num2 < 0.1)
		{
			num2 = 0.1;
		}
		num = MillerChartUpdated.getK23(num2, flowRatio, ConvertAngle(branchAngle), TypeOfTee, out var kStatus);
		if (kStatus != MillerChartStatus.OK && base.ExtrapolationMethod != MillerChartExtrapolationType.None)
		{
			if (base.ExtrapolationMethod == MillerChartExtrapolationType.Area)
			{
				if (kStatus == MillerChartStatus.KaboveMax)
				{
					double num3 = num;
					double num4 = areaRatio;
					do
					{
						num4 *= 1.02;
						num3 = MillerChartUpdated.getK23(num4, flowRatio, ConvertAngle(branchAngle), TypeOfTee, out kStatus);
					}
					while (kStatus != MillerChartStatus.OK && num4 <= 1.0);
					double num5 = num4 + 0.1;
					double k = MillerChartUpdated.getK23(num5, flowRatio, ConvertAngle(branchAngle), TypeOfTee, out kStatus);
					double num6 = (k - num3) / (1.0 / Math.Pow(num5, 2.0) - 1.0 / Math.Pow(num4, 2.0));
					double num7 = num3 - num6 / Math.Pow(num4, 2.0);
					double num8 = num6 / Math.Pow(areaRatio, 2.0) + num7;
					if (num8 > num)
					{
						num = num8;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (base.ExtrapolationMethod == MillerChartExtrapolationType.Gardel)
			{
				num = GardelTeeMethod.getK23(areaRatio, flowRatio, branchAngle, TypeOfTee);
			}
		}
		return num;
	}
}
public class MillerMainSideDPCorrelation : MillerDPCorrelation
{
	internal override double GetMillerChartK(bool AreaLimited, double areaRatio, double flowRatio, double branchAngle, TeeType TypeOfTee)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = areaRatio;
		if (AreaLimited && num2 < 0.1)
		{
			num2 = 0.1;
		}
		num = MillerChartUpdated.getK13(num2, flowRatio, ConvertAngle(branchAngle), TypeOfTee, out var kStatus);
		if (kStatus != MillerChartStatus.OK && base.ExtrapolationMethod != MillerChartExtrapolationType.None)
		{
			if (base.ExtrapolationMethod == MillerChartExtrapolationType.Area)
			{
				if (kStatus == MillerChartStatus.KaboveMax)
				{
					double num3 = num;
					double num4 = areaRatio;
					do
					{
						num4 *= 1.02;
						num3 = MillerChartUpdated.getK13(num4, flowRatio, ConvertAngle(branchAngle), TypeOfTee, out kStatus);
					}
					while (kStatus != MillerChartStatus.OK && num4 <= 1.0);
					double num5 = num4 + 0.1;
					double k = MillerChartUpdated.getK13(num5, flowRatio, ConvertAngle(branchAngle), TypeOfTee, out kStatus);
					double num6 = (k - num3) / (1.0 / Math.Pow(num5, 2.0) - 1.0 / Math.Pow(num4, 2.0));
					double num7 = num3 - num6 / Math.Pow(num4, 2.0);
					double num8 = num6 / Math.Pow(areaRatio, 2.0) + num7;
					if (num8 > num)
					{
						num = num8;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (base.ExtrapolationMethod == MillerChartExtrapolationType.Gardel)
			{
				num = GardelTeeMethod.getK13(areaRatio, flowRatio, branchAngle, TypeOfTee);
			}
		}
		return num;
	}
}
public class GardelMainMainDPCorrelation : TeePressureDropCorrelation
{
	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 0.0;
		if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Combiner)
		{
			num = base.BodyDiameter;
			num2 = CalculateVolumeFlow(base.MainOutletStream, base.BodyDiameter);
		}
		else if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Divider)
		{
			num = base.BodyDiameter;
			num2 = CalculateVolumeFlow(base.MainInletStream, base.BodyDiameter);
		}
		if (num != 0.0 && num2 != 0.0)
		{
			double aratio = base.BranchDiameter * base.BranchDiameter / num / num;
			double num3 = CalculateVolumeFlow(base.BranchStream, base.BranchDiameter);
			double qratio = num3 / num2;
			result = GardelTeeMethod.getK23(aratio, qratio, base.SideAngle, base.TypeOfTee);
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal override IFlowStream GetStreamForDP()
	{
		IFlowStream result = null;
		if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Combiner)
		{
			result = base.MainOutletStream;
		}
		else if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Divider)
		{
			result = base.MainInletStream;
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class GardelMainSideDPCorrelation : TeePressureDropCorrelation
{
	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 0.0;
		if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.YCombiner || base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.YDivider)
		{
			result = 0.0;
		}
		else
		{
			if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Combiner)
			{
				num = base.BodyDiameter;
				num2 = CalculateVolumeFlow(base.MainOutletStream, base.BodyDiameter);
