			else if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Divider)
			{
				num = base.BodyDiameter;
				num2 = CalculateVolumeFlow(base.MainInletStream, base.BodyDiameter);
			}
			if (num != 0.0 && num2 != 0.0)
			{
				double aratio = base.BranchDiameter * base.BranchDiameter / num / num;
				double num3 = CalculateVolumeFlow(base.BranchStream, base.BranchDiameter);
				double qratio = num3 / num2;
				result = GardelTeeMethod.getK13(aratio, qratio, base.SideAngle, base.TypeOfTee);
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal override IFlowStream GetStreamForDP()
	{
		IFlowStream result = null;
		if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Combiner)
		{
			result = base.MainOutletStream;
		}
		else if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.Divider)
		{
			result = base.MainInletStream;
		}
		else if (base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.YCombiner || base.TypeOfTee == TeeType.YDivider)
		{
			result = base.BranchStream;
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class SimpleMainMainDPCorrelation : TeePressureDropCorrelation
{
	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		if (ConvertAngle(base.SideAngle) == TeeAngle._90)
		{
			return 0.38;
		}
		return 0.5;
	}

	internal override IFlowStream GetStreamForDP()
	{
		return mActiveStream;
	}

	public override bool PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return true;
	}
}
public class SimpleMainSideDPCorrelation : TeePressureDropCorrelation
{
	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		if (ConvertAngle(base.SideAngle) == TeeAngle._90)
		{
			return 1.37;
		}
		if (mActiveStream == base.MainOutletStream)
		{
			return 0.76;
		}
		return 1.37;
	}

	internal override IFlowStream GetStreamForDP()
	{
		return mActiveStream;
	}

	public override bool PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return true;
	}
}
public class TulsaUnifiedCorrelation : PipeFlowCorrelation, IPressureGradientCorrelation, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation, IFlowRegimeCorrelation
{
	private double HL;

	private double HW;

	private double HWF;

	private double PGT;

	private double PGA;

	private double PGF;

	private double PGG;

	private int IFGL;

	private int IFOW;

	private double VF;

	private double VC;

	private double HLF;

	private double SI;

	private double SF;

	private double THF;

	private double TH;

	private double TH0;

	private double THW;

	private double FE;

	private double FF;

	private double FI;

	private double HLS;

	private double CU;

	private double CF;

	private double FQN;

	private double RSU;

	private int ICON;

	private double extra_in;

	private FlowRegimeEnum mFlowRegime;

	private FlowRegimeEnum mWaterFlowRegime;

	private double D;

	private double ED;

	private double ANG;

	private double VSG;

	private double VSO;

	private double VSW;

	private double DENG;

	private double DENO;

	private double DENW;

	private double VISG;

	private double VISO;

	private double VISW;

	private double STGO;

	private double STGW;

	private double STOW;

	private double P;

	private int IEM;

	private double VISE;

	private int IPHASE;

	public bool TwoPhaseVersion
	{
		get
		{
			if (IPHASE == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
		set
		{
			if (value)
			{
				IPHASE = 0;
			}
			else
			{
				IPHASE = 1;
			}
		}
	}

	public PressureGradientMethodEnum PressureGradientMethod
	{
		get
		{
			if (IEM == 0)
			{
				return PressureGradientMethodEnum.TulsaUnified3P;
			}
			return PressureGradientMethodEnum.TulsaUnified2P;
		}
	}

	public LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum LiquidHoldUpMethod
	{
		get
		{
			if (IEM == 0)
			{
				return LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum.TulsaUnified3P;
			}
			return LiquidHoldUpMethodEnum.TulsaUnified2P;
		}
	}

	public FlowRegimeMethodEnum FlowRegimeMethod
	{
		get
		{
			if (IEM == 0)
			{
				return FlowRegimeMethodEnum.TulsaUnified3P;
			}
			return FlowRegimeMethodEnum.TulsaUnified2P;
		}
	}

	[DllImport("presdropcorr.dll", CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl)]
	private static extern void GOW_MOD_mp_gow(double D, double ED, double ANG, double VSG, double VSO, double VSW, double DENG, double DENO, double DENW, double VISG, double VISO, double VISW, double STGO, double STGW, double STOW, double P, ref double HL, ref double HW, ref double HWF, ref double PGT, ref double PGF, ref double PGG, ref double PGA, ref int IFGL, ref int IFOW, ref double VF, ref double VC, ref double HLF, ref double SF, ref double SI, ref double THF, ref double TH, ref double TH0, ref double THW, ref double FE, ref double FF, ref double FI, ref double HLS, ref double CU, ref double CF, ref double FQN, ref double RSU, ref int ICON, int IEM, ref double VISE, ref double extra_in);

	public TulsaUnifiedCorrelation()
	{
		if (PressureGradientMethod == PressureGradientMethodEnum.TulsaUnified3P)
		{
			IPHASE = 1;
		}
		else
		{
			IPHASE = 0;
		}
	}

	public TulsaUnifiedCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double diam, double angle, double roughness)
		: base(inStream, diam, angle, roughness)
	{
		if (PressureGradientMethod == PressureGradientMethodEnum.TulsaUnified3P)
		{
			IPHASE = 1;
		}
		else
		{
			IPHASE = 0;
		}
	}

	internal bool NeedToRecalculate()
	{
		bool result = false;
		IPhase phaseByType = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
		IPhase phaseByType2 = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
		IPhase phaseByType3 = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous);
		if (phaseByType2 != null)
		{
			if (VSG != GasSupVelocity())
			{
				VSG = GasSupVelocity();
				result = true;
			}
			if (DENG != phaseByType2.Density.Value)
			{
				DENG = phaseByType2.Density.Value;
				result = true;
			}
			if (VISG != phaseByType2.Viscosity.Value)
			{
				VISG = phaseByType2.Viscosity.Value;
				result = true;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if (VSG != 0.0 || DENG != 0.0 || VISG != 0.0)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			VSG = 0.0;
			DENG = 0.0;
			VISG = 0.0;
		}
		if (phaseByType != null)
		{
			if (VSO != LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: false))
			{
				VSO = LiquidSupVelocity(combinedPhase: false);
				result = true;
			}
			if (DENO != phaseByType.Density.Value)
			{
				DENO = phaseByType.Density.Value;
				result = true;
			}
			if (VISO != phaseByType.Viscosity.Value)
			{
				VISO = phaseByType.Viscosity.Value;
				result = true;
			}
			if (STGO != phaseByType.SurfaceTension.Value)
			{
				STGO = phaseByType.SurfaceTension.Value;
				result = true;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if (VSO != 0.0 || DENO != 0.0 || VISO != 0.0 || STGO != 0.0)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			VSO = 0.0;
			DENO = 0.0;
			VISO = 0.0;
			STGO = 0.0;
		}
		if (phaseByType3 != null)
		{
			if (VSW != WaterSupVelocity())
			{
				VSW = WaterSupVelocity();
				result = true;
			}
			if (DENW != phaseByType3.Density.Value)
			{
				DENW = phaseByType3.Density.Value;
				result = true;
			}
			if (VISW != phaseByType3.Viscosity.Value)
			{
				VISW = phaseByType3.Viscosity.Value;
				result = true;
			}
			if (STGW != phaseByType3.SurfaceTension.Value)
			{
				STGW = phaseByType3.SurfaceTension.Value;
				result = true;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if (VSW != 0.0 || DENW != 0.0 || VISW != 0.0 || STGW != 0.0)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			VSW = 0.0;
			DENW = 1000.0;
			VISW = 0.001;
			STGW = 0.0728;
		}
		if (P != base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value)
		{
			P = base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
			result = true;
		}
		if (D != base.Diameter)
		{
			D = base.Diameter;
			result = true;
		}
		if (ANG != base.InclinedAngle)
		{
			ANG = base.InclinedAngle;
			result = true;
		}
		if (ED != base.Roughness / base.Diameter)
		{
			ED = base.Roughness / base.Diameter;
			result = true;
		}
		if (base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase != null && VISE != base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value)
		{
			VISE = base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			result = true;
		}
		return result;
	}

	internal void ClearResults()
	{
		HL = 0.0;
		HW = 0.0;
		HWF = 0.0;
		PGT = 0.0;
		PGA = 0.0;
		PGF = 0.0;
		PGG = 0.0;
		IFGL = 0;
		IFOW = 0;
		VF = 0.0;
		VC = 0.0;
		HLF = 0.0;
		SI = 0.0;
		SF = 0.0;
		THF = 0.0;
		TH = 0.0;
		TH0 = 0.0;
		THW = 0.0;
		FE = 0.0;
		FF = 0.0;
		FI = 0.0;
		HLS = 0.0;
		CU = 0.0;
		CF = 0.0;
		FQN = 0.0;
		RSU = 0.0;
		ICON = 0;
		mFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		extra_in = 0.0;
	}

	internal void ExecuteCalculation()
	{
		if (base.FlowStream == null || base.Diameter == 0.0)
		{
			ClearResults();
		}
		else
		{
			if (!NeedToRecalculate())
			{
				return;
			}
			IEM = -2;
			extra_in = 0.0;
			STOW = 0.05;
			if (IPHASE == 0)
			{
				IEM = -1;
				if (VSO > VSW)
				{
					STGW = STGO;
				}
				else if (VSW > VSO)
				{
					STGO = STGW;
				}
			}
			GOW_MOD_mp_gow(D, ED, ANG, VSG, VSO, VSW, DENG, DENO, DENW, VISG, VISO, VISW, STGO, STGW, STOW, P, ref HL, ref HW, ref HWF, ref PGT, ref PGF, ref PGG, ref PGA, ref IFGL, ref IFOW, ref VF, ref VC, ref HLF, ref SF, ref SI, ref THF, ref TH, ref TH0, ref THW, ref FE, ref FF, ref FI, ref HLS, ref CU, ref CF, ref FQN, ref RSU, ref ICON, IEM, ref VISE, ref extra_in);
			if (IPHASE == 1)
			{
				HL -= HW;
			}
			else
			{
				HW = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			}
			switch (IFGL)
			{
			case 0:
				mFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
				break;
			case 1:
				mFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Liquid;
				break;
			case 2:
				mFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.DispersedBubble;
				break;
			case 3:
				mFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Bubble;
				break;
			case 4:
				mFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Intermittent;
				break;
			case 5:
				mFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Stratified;
				break;
			case 6:
				mFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Annular;
				break;
			case 7:
				mFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Vapour;
				break;
			default:
				mFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
				break;
			}
			switch (IFOW)
			{
			case 0:
				mWaterFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
				break;
			case 1:
				mWaterFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Liquid;
				break;
			case 2:
				mWaterFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.DispersedBubble;
				break;
			case 3:
				mWaterFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Bubble;
				break;
			case 4:
				mWaterFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Intermittent;
				break;
			case 5:
				mWaterFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Stratified;
				break;
			case 6:
				mWaterFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Annular;
				break;
			case 7:
				mWaterFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Vapour;
				break;
			default:
				mWaterFlowRegime = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
				break;
			}
			mUsedReynoldsNum = CombinedLiquidHomogeneousDensity() * (GasSupVelocity() + LiquidSupVelocity(TwoPhaseVersion)) * base.Diameter / CombinedLiquidHomogeneousViscosity();
			mUsedFrictionFactor = FF;
		}
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		double num = 0.0;
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		double num2 = CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		double num3 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		double num4 = CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
		return num2 + num3 + num4;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		return CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		ExecuteCalculation();
		return PGF;
	}

	public bool AccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return true;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		return CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		ExecuteCalculation();
		return PGG;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		return CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(liqHoldCorr, pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient(ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr, FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		ExecuteCalculation();
		return PGA;
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		return CalculateLiquidHoldUp(pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateLiquidHoldUp(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		ExecuteCalculation();
		return HL;
	}

	public double CalculateWaterHoldUp()
	{
		if (IPHASE == 1)
		{
			ExecuteCalculation();
			return HW;
		}
		return RealValue.FEMPTY;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		return CalculateOverallDensity(pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		ExecuteCalculation();
		return CombinedLiquidHoldupDensity(HL);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(IFlowRegimeCorrelation flowRegCorr)
	{
		FlowRegimeEnum pattern = FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown;
		return CalculateEquivalentDensity(pattern);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum pattern)
	{
		ExecuteCalculation();
		return CombinedLiquidHoldupDensity(HL);
	}

	public FlowRegimeEnum EstimateFlowPattern()
	{
		ExecuteCalculation();
		return mFlowRegime;
	}

	public FlowRegimeEnum EstimateWaterFlowPattern()
	{
		ExecuteCalculation();
		return mWaterFlowRegime;
	}
}
public class UserFrictionFactorCorrelation : ICorrelationManager, IFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private IFlowStream mStream;

	private double mInDiameter;

	private double mOutDiameter;

	private double mKFactor;

	protected ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation;

	public double InletDiameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mInDiameter;
		}
		set
		{
			mInDiameter = value;
		}
	}

	public double OutletDiameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mOutDiameter;
		}
		set
		{
			mOutDiameter = value;
		}
	}

	public ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation LiquidHoldUpCorrelation
	{
		get
		{
			return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation;
		}
		set
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = value;
		}
	}

	public double UserFrictionFactor
	{
		set
		{
			mKFactor = value;
		}
	}

	public IFlowStream FlowStream
	{
		get
		{
			return mStream;
		}
		set
		{
			mStream = value;
			if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation is PipeFlowCorrelation)
			{
				(mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation as PipeFlowCorrelation).FlowStream = mStream;
			}
		}
	}

	public UserFrictionFactorCorrelation()
	{
		mStream = null;
		mInDiameter = 0.0;
		mOutDiameter = 0.0;
		mKFactor = 0.0;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = null;
	}

	public UserFrictionFactorCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double kFactor, double inDiam, double outDiam)
	{
		mKFactor = kFactor;
		mStream = inStream;
		mInDiameter = inDiam;
		mOutDiameter = outDiam;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, inDiam, 0.0, 0.0);
	}

	public UserFrictionFactorCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double kFactor, double inDiam, double outDiam, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldCorr)
	{
		mKFactor = kFactor;
		mStream = inStream;
		mInDiameter = inDiam;
		mOutDiameter = outDiam;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldCorr;
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient()
	{
		double num = CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
		double num3 = CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
		return num + num2 + num3;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (mStream != null && mInDiameter > 0.0 && mOutDiameter > 0.0)
		{
			double num = CalculateKFactor();
			double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(Math.Min(mInDiameter, mOutDiameter), 2.0);
			double num3 = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num2;
			result = -0.5 * num * num3 * num3 / CalculateOverallDensity();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity()
	{
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation == null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		}
		return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity()
	{
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation == null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		}
		return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public bool PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return false;
	}

	public double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		return mKFactor;
	}
}
public abstract class ValveFrictionFactorCorrelation : ICorrelationManager, IFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private IFlowStream mStream;

	private double mDiameter;

	private double mThroat;

	private double mInAngle;

	protected ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation;

	public double Diameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mDiameter;
		}
		set
		{
			mDiameter = value;
		}
	}

	public double ThroatDiameter
	{
		get
		{
			return mThroat;
		}
		set
		{
			mThroat = value;
		}
	}

	public double InletAngle
	{
		get
		{
			return mInAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mInAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation LiquidHoldUpCorrelation
	{
		get
		{
			return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation;
		}
		set
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = value;
		}
	}

	public IFlowStream FlowStream
	{
		get
		{
			return mStream;
		}
		set
		{
			mStream = value;
			if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation is PipeFlowCorrelation)
			{
				(mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation as PipeFlowCorrelation).FlowStream = mStream;
			}
		}
	}

	public ValveFrictionFactorCorrelation()
	{
		mStream = null;
		mDiameter = 0.0;
		mThroat = 0.0;
		mInAngle = 0.0;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = null;
	}

	public ValveFrictionFactorCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle)
	{
		mStream = inStream;
		mDiameter = mainDiam;
		mThroat = throatDiam;
		mInAngle = inAngle;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = null;
	}

	public double CalculatePressureGradient()
	{
		double num = CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient();
		double num2 = CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient();
		double num3 = CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient();
		return num + num2 + num3;
	}

	public double CalculateFrictionalPressureGradient()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (mStream != null && mDiameter > 0.0)
		{
			double num = CalculateKFactor();
			double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(mDiameter, 2.0);
			double num3 = mStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num2;
			result = -0.5 * num * num3 * num3 / CalculateEquivalentDensity();
		}
		return result;
	}

	public double CalculateGravitationalPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateAccelerationPressureGradient()
	{
		return 0.0;
	}

	public double CalculateOverallDensity()
	{
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation == null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		}
		return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateOverallDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public double CalculateEquivalentDensity()
	{
		if (mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation == null)
		{
			mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(mStream, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		}
		return mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.CalculateEquivalentDensity(FlowRegimeEnum.Unknown);
	}

	public bool PressureGradientAccountsForAcceleration()
	{
		return false;
	}

	public abstract double CalculateKFactor();
}
public class GateValveCraneFrictionCorrelation : ValveFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private double mOpening;

	public double Opening
	{
		get
		{
			return mOpening;
		}
		set
		{
			mOpening = value;
		}
	}

	public GateValveCraneFrictionCorrelation()
	{
		mOpening = 1.0;
	}

	public GateValveCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle, double openness)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpening = openness;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, mainDiam, 0.0, 0.0);
	}

	public GateValveCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle, double openness, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpening = openness;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.Diameter > 0.0)
		{
			double num = Crane.FrictionFactorInTurbulenceZone(base.Diameter);
			double num2 = 8.0 * num;
			double num3 = base.ThroatDiameter / base.Diameter;
			result = ((num3 == 1.0) ? num2 : ((!(base.InletAngle < 45.0)) ? Crane.KFactorFormula6(num3, num2, HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.InletAngle)) : Crane.KFactorFormula5(num3, num2, HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.InletAngle))));
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class GateValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation : ValveFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private double mOpening;

	public double Opening
	{
		get
		{
			return mOpening;
		}
		set
		{
			mOpening = value;
		}
	}

	public GateValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation()
	{
		mOpening = 1.0;
	}

	public GateValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle, double openness)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpening = openness;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, mainDiam, 0.0, 0.0, HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.FittingType.Valve);
	}

	public GateValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle, double openness, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpening = openness;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.Diameter > 0.0 && base.FlowStream != null)
		{
			double viscosity = ((base.FlowStream.NumberOfPhases >= 2) ? base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value : base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Viscosity.Value);
			double num = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(base.Diameter, 2.0);
			double massFlux = base.FlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num;
			result = SinglePhaseFlow.KFactorForGateValve(base.Diameter, base.ThroatDiameter, massFlux, Opening, viscosity);
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class GlobeValveCraneFrictionCorrelation : ValveFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private double mOpening;

	public double Opening
	{
		get
		{
			return mOpening;
		}
		set
		{
			mOpening = value;
		}
	}

	public GlobeValveCraneFrictionCorrelation()
	{
		mOpening = 1.0;
	}

	public GlobeValveCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle, double openness)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpening = openness;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, mainDiam, 0.0, 0.0);
	}

	public GlobeValveCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle, double openness, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpening = openness;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.Diameter > 0.0)
		{
			double num = Crane.FrictionFactorInTurbulenceZone(base.Diameter);
			double num2 = 340.0 * num;
			double num3 = base.ThroatDiameter / base.Diameter;
			result = ((num3 != 1.0) ? Crane.KFactorFormula7(num3, num2, HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.InletAngle)) : num2);
		}
		return result;
	}
}

public class BallValveCraneFrictionCorrelation : ValveFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private double mOpenAngle;

	private double mBlindAngle;

	public double OpenAngle
	{
		get
		{
			return mOpenAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mOpenAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public double BlindAngle
	{
		get
		{
			return mBlindAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mBlindAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public BallValveCraneFrictionCorrelation()
	{
		mOpenAngle = 90.0;
		mBlindAngle = 10.0;
	}

	public BallValveCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle, double openAngle, double blindAngle)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpenAngle = openAngle;
		mBlindAngle = blindAngle;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, mainDiam, 0.0, 0.0);
	}

	public BallValveCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle, double openAngle, double blindAngle, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpenAngle = openAngle;
		mBlindAngle = blindAngle;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.Diameter > 0.0)
		{
			double num = Crane.FrictionFactorInTurbulenceZone(base.Diameter);
			double num2 = 3.0 * num;
			double num3 = base.ThroatDiameter / base.Diameter;
			result = ((num3 == 1.0) ? num2 : ((!(base.InletAngle < 45.0)) ? Crane.KFactorFormula6(num3, num2, HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.InletAngle)) : Crane.KFactorFormula5(num3, num2, HTFSUtility.DegreesToRads(base.InletAngle))));
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class BallValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation : ValveFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private double mOpenAngle;

	private double mBlindAngle;

	public double OpenAngle
	{
		get
		{
			return mOpenAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mOpenAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public double BlindAngle
	{
		get
		{
			return mBlindAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mBlindAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public BallValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation()
	{
		mOpenAngle = 90.0;
		mBlindAngle = 10.0;
	}

	public BallValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle, double openAngle, double blindAngle)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpenAngle = openAngle;
		mBlindAngle = blindAngle;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, mainDiam, 0.0, 0.0, HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.FittingType.Valve);
	}

	public BallValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double throatDiam, double inAngle, double openAngle, double blindAngle, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, throatDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpenAngle = openAngle;
		mBlindAngle = blindAngle;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.Diameter > 0.0 && base.FlowStream != null)
		{
			double viscosity = ((base.FlowStream.NumberOfPhases >= 2) ? base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value : base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Viscosity.Value);
			double num = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(base.Diameter, 2.0);
			double massFlux = base.FlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num;

		}

	}

public class ButterflyValveCraneFrictionCorrelation : ValveFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private double mOpenAngle;

	private double mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio;

	public double OpenAngle
	{
		get
		{
			return mOpenAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mOpenAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public double DiscToPipeDiameterRatio
	{
		get
		{
			return mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio;
		}
		set
		{
			mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio = value;
		}
	}

	public ButterflyValveCraneFrictionCorrelation()
	{
		mOpenAngle = 90.0;
		mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio = 1.0;
	}

	public ButterflyValveCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double inAngle, double openAngle, double discRatio)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, mainDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpenAngle = openAngle;
		mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio = discRatio;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HomogeneousLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, mainDiam, 0.0, 0.0);
	}

	public ButterflyValveCraneFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double inAngle, double openAngle, double discRatio, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, mainDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpenAngle = openAngle;
		mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio = discRatio;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.Diameter > 0.0)
		{
			double num = Crane.FrictionFactorInTurbulenceZone(base.Diameter);
			result = ((base.Diameter < 0.2) ? (45.0 * num) : ((!(base.Diameter < 0.4)) ? (25.0 * num) : (35.0 * num)));
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class ButterflyValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation : ValveFrictionFactorCorrelation
{
	private double mOpenAngle;

	private double mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio;

	public double OpenAngle
	{
		get
		{
			return mOpenAngle;
		}
		set
		{
			mOpenAngle = value;
		}
	}

	public double DiscToPipeDiameterRatio
	{
		get
		{
			return mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio;
		}
		set
		{
			mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio = value;
		}
	}

	public ButterflyValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation()
	{
		mOpenAngle = 90.0;
		mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio = 1.0;
	}

	public ButterflyValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double inAngle, double openAngle, double discRatio)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, mainDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpenAngle = openAngle;
		mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio = discRatio;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = new HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation(inStream, mainDiam, 0.0, 0.0, HTFSLiquidHoldUpCorrelation.FittingType.Valve);
	}

	public ButterflyValveHTFSFrictionCorrelation(IFlowStream inStream, double mainDiam, double inAngle, double openAngle, double discRatio, ILiquidHoldUpCorrelation liqHoldUpCorr)
		: base(inStream, mainDiam, mainDiam, inAngle)
	{
		mOpenAngle = openAngle;
		mDiscToPipeDiameterRatio = discRatio;
		mLiquidHoldUpCorrelation = liqHoldUpCorr;
	}

	public override double CalculateKFactor()
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (base.Diameter > 0.0 && base.FlowStream != null)
		{
			double viscosity = ((base.FlowStream.NumberOfPhases >= 2) ? base.FlowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase.Viscosity.Value : base.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Viscosity.Value);
			double num = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(base.Diameter, 2.0);
			double massFlux = base.FlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num;
			result = SinglePhaseFlow.KFactorForButterflyValve(base.Diameter, DiscToPipeDiameterRatio, massFlux, OpenAngle, viscosity);
		}
		return result;
	}
}
